# Audit of 2026-09-23

Two reviews run on claude-opus-5-5 against the working tree: every place the
added species (508..1437) can overflow a table, archive or guard, and every
ported area against konefr's reference, each claim put to a refuter that
tried to disprove it. What is below survived. A row closes by a commit that
names it; the ledger's rows point here where they are affected.

## Found by playing the gyms headlessly

- **high** Whitney and Morty hang the battle. The battle asks for row 1419
  (tag nickname, the opposing battler) and the reader asserts; the text
  printer never finishes. The first reading of it was wrong: it is not
  msg_0197 and not a reference row number. The printer is ov12_0225A85C, the
  task that builds "X used Y!" as row 3 * move + side of msg_0003, and that
  bank stopped at Shadow Force, 467 -- `import_moves.py` wrote the names,
  capitals and descriptions of every added move and never this bank. Morty's
  Misdreavus uses Hex, 473, and 3 * 473 = 1419; any move past 467, on either
  side, stopped a battle the same way. -- fixed: the importer writes the
  three rows for all 456 moves past retail, as the reference's movedatagen
  does, and `test_message_banks.py` holds the bank to the move count. Morty
  and Whitney now play to the end with no assertion.
- Falkner played to the end headlessly: the leader's Super Potion, Delibird's
  Focus Sash, the badge, the prize, and the party's experience held at the
  cap. Bugsy played to the end (the player's party lost to Scizor and
  Heracross, no error). -- verified

## Second audit: retail limits the expansion crossed

Asked for by Paolo the same evening and run by the other session: six
finders looking for more bugs of the ledger's silent family -- a limit
retail baked in, crossed by the expansion, failing with no build or test
error -- then every item re-derived here by an adversarial verifier on
8b0eb2d7e. Two did not survive and are not rows: the Wi-Fi Plaza footprint
table (every path clamps species past 493 to Ditto before reading it) and
move target 24 (Rototiller, Flower Shield and Teatime take the same branches
as in the reference, where they are marked unusable).

- **critical** *FIGHT button of a Fairy move* -- `ov06_0221BA00` indexed an 18-entry palette table by the move's type; Fairy (18) read the 0 after it and loaded a palette from address 0. A data abort on hardware as FIGHT opens with any Fairy move (a wild Lillipup on Route 34 has Baby-Doll Eyes). -- fixed in 62b0da55e
- **critical** *Dex and capture type icon of a Fairy species* -- `ov18_021F967C`/`ov18_021F9688` indexed 18-entry tables; Fairy loaded zukan_gra member 0, a screen file, as the icon: NULL character data on the capture page, a read of 0x14 on the Dex page. -- fixed in bc8e8908b and 38d79dcb8
- **medium** *evolving into a form species registers nothing* -- `DexSpeciesIsInvalid` (src/pokedex.c) rejects species past NATIONAL_DEX_COUNT and nothing maps a form to its base; a female Litleo evolving into PYROAR_FEMALE (1312) leaves Pyroar unseen and uncaught, while the unique-caught stat still counts it. Needs a form-to-base table from the importer and one lookup in the Dex's seen/caught paths. -- fixed in 6f58a88f6
- **medium** *Battle Frontier abilities in a byte* -- `FrontierMon.ability` (include/scrcmd_9.h) is a u8 written from the u16 ability and written back with SetMonData: Weezing's Neutralizing Gas becomes none, Gallade's Sharpness Trace, Sudowoodo's Irrigation Minus. Bit 31 of the IV word is free for the high byte. -- fixed in 499ff7de9
- **medium** *Metronome rolls moves 1..467 only* -- `BtlCmd_Metronome` takes `% 0x1d3`; the reference rolls over all its moves, with its ban list widened for the added uncallable ones. -- fixed in 0ac85fa23
- **medium** *battle videos with added content are refused, playback stops at a move past 467* -- `sub_0203018C` (asm/unk_0202FBCC.s) checks species, items and moves against retail's limits, and `ov12_0225E4EC` against 0x1D3. Both need a matching decompilation first. -- fixed in e87865d7f (matching) and 4b42a09c0
- **low** *field abilities in a byte* -- `EncounterGenState.ability` (src/field/encounter_check.c) and `GetEggCyclesToSubtract` (src/get_egg.c) keep the ability in a u8, so an ability past 255 acts as a retail one: Farigiraf's Cud Chew (291) doubles the encounter rate as Illuminate, Armor Tail (296) halves egg cycles as Magma Armor. -- fixed in 46d9c9c52
- **low** *National Dex completion needs 1018 species* -- ea74ddf71 made `Pokedex_NationalDexIsComplete` count 1..1041 less the gap and nine mythicals; Oak's rating still calls the Dex complete past 483, while the diploma and the trainer card's star can never be earned. The reference leaves the completion at retail's count. -- fixed in f8ececb71
- **low** *AI replacement picker reads retail-sized move data* -- `ov12_02258800` reads a move's power by raw offset into `moveData[NUM_MOVES + 1]`; for an added move it reads an unrelated context byte. Needs a matching decompilation first; lands with the overlay-10 AI rows above. -- fixed in 7a3cb630e (matching) and c9311e949
- **low** *Pokemon Talk never mentions an added species* -- src/application/pokegear/radio/shows/pokemon_talk.c filters encounter slots by `MAX_SPECIES` (493). -- fixed in be9922c3a
- **low** *photo caption counts species 1..493* -- src/application/view_photo.c:480, the C twin of the open overlay-109 row. -- fixed in e252d3cdb (the C viewer; the overlay-109 twin is its own open row)
- **low** *mail shows every added species as the placeholder icon* -- src/mail.c clamps an icon index past 546 to 7. -- fixed in 76f548f36
- **low** *Battle Castle summary names the wrong ability past 255* -- `Ov83MonSummary.ability` is a u8; the battle itself uses the full ability. -- fixed in 78a539fda
- **low** *evolution record read into 48 bytes, 50 written* -- the template's `u16 pad0` after eight evolutions makes each evo.narc member 50 bytes, and both readers size 48; two zero bytes land past the buffer on every evolution and Eviolite check, harmlessly by luck of layout. -- fixed in be2573c48
- **low** *Trainer House record keeps the ability in a byte* -- include/save_trainer_house.h; reached only through an IR record. -- fixed in cde578b69
- **low** *Wi-Fi Club refuses a partner with an added species or item* -- src/overlay_44_0222CDAC.c `ov44_0222DFEC` keeps retail's bounds. Online only. -- fixed in 549cdc727
- **low** *update_vanilla_species.py --write would erase 451 hidden abilities* -- it rebuilds each record with the hidden ability defaulted to none. -- fixed in 3a96373ca
- **medium** *an evolution never teaches its evolution move* -- 272 learnset entries at level 0 in 268 species (two of them konefr's: Ariados' Swords Dance, Sudowoodo's Slam) mean "learned on evolution" in the reference, which keys on the evolution scene's call; here `MonTryLearnMoveOnLevelUp` only matches the current level. The evolution scene (`sub_02075E14`, asm/unk_020755E8.s) needs a matching decompilation first. -- fixed in 8042523bc (matching) and c1f868514
- **low** *no machine bit for a move learnt by level-up* -- the reference's build_learnsets.py also sets a species' machine bit when it learns the machine's move by level; no importer here does, so Delibird cannot take TM51 Roost, Sudowoodo TM81 X-Scissor. The machines past HM08 are inert either way (the open items row). -- fixed in 307fa1ef0 (engine) and f4db797d2 (konefr's Delibird and Sudowoodo)
- **low** *Dex number '?12' past 1000* -- the summary and the PC format the Dex number with three digits; a female Pyroar or Meowstic (1312, 1313) prints '?12' with the National Dex on. The reference widens the six digit counts to four; the root cause is the same missing form-to-base map as above. -- fixed in 311eca757 for every species a player can have: a form prints its base species' number (female Pyroar 549); the four-digit widening is its own open row
- **low** *bag lists 165 items, 24 balls, 50 key items* -- overlay 15's pocket counts and its 165 name strings are retail's while the save pockets were widened; not fillable by today's content. Needs the bag's list builder decompiled. The list builder and its strings are C since f86d8d6fe (matching); the redraw ov15_021FF364, ov15_021FF4EC, ov15_02200140, Bag_Init and the two size tables are left, then the reference's sizes. -- fixed in 81596de42 and 29d43db7b; the Items pocket's 197 slots seen listed and named in the harness
- **low** *jsonproc archives do not depend on their headers* -- personal, evo, trainer, encounter and tutor rules list only their JSON and template; a changed constant or record layout leaves the archive stale in an incremental build. All six rebuild byte-identical today. -- fixed in 0450c7d6f (the gaps it found are their own open row)
- **low** *Dex list builder sized for 493* -- `ov18_021F7ED4` and its neighbours in overlay 18 keep 493-entry scratch lists, a 518-slot grid and a 494-byte filter file; latent until the sort lists grow, with the open sort-list and PokedexAppData rows. The route, from round 5's reading: the two PokedexAppData lists become heap buffers of NATIONAL_DEX_COUNT as the reference's pokedex.s does, after seventeen overlay-18 accessors are decompiled matching; the Dex heap, zukan_hw_data_1 and the grid's three tile digits follow. -- fixed in 61cb37ad9, 0392bbab1, c131074b9, d0033e401, cb73ad79b and 806f55fbf; with the other fourteen matching accessors of that round; three that read only the counts stay asm on purpose
- **low** *Pokeathlon medal viewer asserts species <= 493* -- `ov99_021E72C0`; latent until the sort lists grow, with the open medal rows. -- closed (Paolo, 2026-09-23): added species do not earn Pokeathlon medals for now; a medal won by one is not recorded
- **low** *egg moves searched in 2045 halfwords, 16 a species* -- src/get_egg.c; latent until the egg moves are imported (the open daycare row). -- fixed in 0d9b59745
- **low** *Pokewalker receive path clears species past 493* -- five checks in overlay 112 the reference raises; IR only, and the send side is broken first (the open Pokewalker rows). -- out of scope (Paolo, 2026-09-23): the Pokewalker needs infrared, which no emulator or flashcart has; the plan is in the row
- **low** *two obtainable items' plurals do not fit* -- hg-engine's plurals 'pieces of Never-Melt Ice' and 'doses of Secret Medicine' are 33 units against the 32 of a MessageFormat buffer; tossing more than one Never-Melt Ice shows the bag's previous word. The reference has the same text and buffer. `test_text_length.py` lists both as known, so a third fails. -- fixed in c99abb1e3
- **low** *four-digit Dex numbers* -- species 1000..1041 print '?xx' with the three digits six sites format; the reference widens them to four. Needs sub_0208CC88, ov14_021F5190, ov18_021EE75C, ov18_021EEC34 and ov18_021F8CCC decompiled first. No player can have those species yet. -- fixed in 03f377af4, 551ba0d5e, 56e351cf8, c66e33f80 (matching) and fd6571659; seen in the harness as 'Dex No. 0023', 'No.0023' and '0025 Pikachu'
- **low** *build rules the jsonproc fix did not reach* -- zone_event bins do not depend on the headers their json names; safari_enc.mk's explicit rule writes '.s' and '.o' in the repository root; personal.mk has a dead line; clean-zukan-enc leaves the next full build failing until zukan_enc.naix is regenerated. -- fixed in bb85fc556, d27362d46, cf1eca6a8 and d27d143fa
- **low** *Shellos and Gastrodon East Sea register as West* -- form species map to their base with form 0 now; no encounter, trainer or script gives either. -- fixed in c6c6751e0
- **medium** *retail moves: what is still not hg-engine's* -- after 069b260e4 the retail records carry hg-engine's type, power, accuracy, PP, chance and named flags, with these left: Beat Up's power (retail's formula multiplies it), Sweet Scent's and Howl's effects (the scripts are retail's stubs or single-target), seven priorities (Teleport, Protect, Detect, Endure, Extreme Speed, Fake Out, Follow Me) and four targets (Poison Gas, Cotton Spore, Conversion 2, Howl); Knock Off has hg-engine's 65 but not its x1.5 against an item, Rapid Spin its 50 and 100% but not the Speed raise. -- fixed in 39525f093, 718e227e5, 4e1735d4c, 0de9d864e, d569bcb95, 84e2ae29c, fdbacf8e4 and 770e8b666; Conversion 2 keeps its retail target on purpose, because the engine's spread target with Gen 4's command would hit every neighbour -- a row of its own
- **medium** *retail effect scripts differ between the two trees* -- about 120 of effect scripts 0..276 differ in text at d0380a487: some only in flag order, many for real -- the stubs 21/22/61/63/64 filled with stat drops, 20 made a two-stage drop, Explosion/Damp, the binding moves, Rapid Spin and Beat Up reworked. import_moves.py's premise that they are "the same file in both trees" holds only in part. Fifteen brought over in 4c2036ec5, cd8841b33, abc652f92, 3f3b14aa3, 9159c3f40, 9c5ba5921, ddefec441, 8b65c721b, d569bcb95 and ff8fa9e34; c44f17475 compares the rest, and the ones still different are STILL_DIFFERENT in test_retail_effect_scripts.py, each with what it waits on -- the engine's C passes, the entry-hazard queue, Future Sight. Round 6 brought ten more (the hazard queue and the moves' power worked out with the damage: 307d2db57, 84854579a, 370835844, 608cff102, a49e3810a, f8fb6c5a7 and 1efb8032b); 36 are left, each with its real reason (40ef777e6 and ce05027fe), most waiting on the engine's post-move C. The seventh round made the engine's post-move steps C, in its order (recoil, the additional effects, Magician, the Red Card and Eject Button, Pickpocket, Emergency Exit, U-turn's switch), and brought Reckless and the recoil moves, Smelling Salts and Wake-Up Slap, the binding moves, Knock Off, Thief and Covet, Pluck and Bug Bite, U-turn and Fling over (8310f2152 to 54868614f), 36 left to 23; 19 of them are deliberate differences written down with their reason, and four wait on C outside the post-move pass: 7 Explosion (Damp before the move, the faint in the post-move step), 151 and 272 (the charge turn and the Power Herb), 222 Natural Gift (its power, a failure check before the script, the Berry spent on a miss too). -- fixed in 71ac3ac52 for the last two, 151 Solar Beam and 272 Shadow Force: the charge turn and the Power Herb are the controller's, as the engine asks them before the move (TryChargeTurn, in place of the move script, and subscript 473: the attack message, then the charge, or the herb and the hit); 272 is the engine's script, and 151 the engine's but for its sun path, a deliberate difference STILL_DIFFERENT keeps with its reason (it would play the animation a second time here and show Mega Sol's popup off the raw ability). The decision is the latest games' rule, the engine's subscripts 422 and 426 and Showdown's move line before its -prepare: seen in the headless harness, 'Shuckle used Solar Beam!' then 'Shuckle absorbed light!', 'used Shadow Force!' then 'vanished instantly!' with the sprite hidden, and after Sunny Day 'absorbed light!' and the hit in the same turn; the Power Herb by a host test only, a Furret knocking the herb off first. 7 and 222 are the engine's since the ninth round, their logic in C (74a1d0ee8 and a40a886d7 for Damp and the explosions' faint before the move, after 3e3be5d1c; 04caf40ee, ee74b91ab and c97826978 for Natural Gift worked out before the move and its Berry spent once the move is over). The other charge moves are a row of the tenth round
- **low** *item names longer than their buffers* -- 74 item names are longer than the bag's 18-unit list strings, 16 than the trade screen's 20, 31 than the Pokewalker's 19; 237 descriptions than the bag's and shop's 130, 64 plurals than the 32-unit MessageFormat. A copy that does not fit is refused and the screen keeps the previous string. None of those items can be obtained today. The bag's list names, the plurals and the three description buffers were widened in 802579d70, b7791a5c7 and 243be6fda (after f86d8d6fe and b3f8af7f0, matching); the trade screen's String_New(0x14) in ov65_0221D674, still asm, is what is left. -- fixed in 404e91e38 and 555c41663; with the bag's, the plurals' and the descriptions' of round 5
- **low** *heap margins after the per-species tables grew* -- `PokedexData_LoadAll` takes 23,008 bytes (retail 7,904) from the battle heap at every switch-in and from the field heap in the regulation menu. No allocation failed through four gym fights, but nothing measures the margin. -- closed: measured in d69344404 -- the battle heap keeps 240,300 bytes at its fullest in Morty's fight against the 23,008 a switch-in loads, FIELD2 64,656; the heaps that are close are a row of their own

## Found while fixing, 2026-09-23 night

What the agents of the third round found in passing, re-read here before
being written down. Each is engine layer unless it says otherwise.

- **critical** *four status subscripts longer than the script buffer* -- the third round's immunities grew Bad Poison, Poison, Fall Asleep and Burn past retail's 1600-byte battleScriptBuffer, so every load wrote into the fields after it; caught by the size assertion in the gym replay (Falkner's Toxic Orb). -- fixed in c9b5c2ec9, hg-engine's 650-word buffer at the end of BattleContext
- **critical** *opening the PC wrote 12 KB past its allocation* -- the box app's graphics state was retail's 0x88E0 bytes while thirty box pictures of 0x400 run from +0x40C8 to 0xB8C8, over the state's last fields and into what heap 10 allocates next; hg-engine grows the state and moves the pictures. -- fixed in ba1936a9a (matching) and 1d92c1b9e; whether heap 10 has room for the larger state is its own row
- **low** *heap 10 and the larger PC state* -- the state grew by about 30 KB and heap 10 (0x80000) did not, as in the reference; not yet opened on an emulator. -- closed: measured in d69344404 -- 238,612 bytes left at its fullest, the PC opened in Violet City and paged through
- **medium** *the Safari Game counted 18 boxes* -- BtlCmd_CheckSafariGameDone compared the first box with room with a literal 18. -- fixed in 3f9864d55
- **low** *the trainer AI's protect-chain command counts three moves* -- ov10_0221EBAC loads a chain only after Protect, Detect or Endure, not after the shields or the team guards. -- fixed in c91754f56 (matching) and 2116e7600
- **medium** *CheckSubstitute ignores Infiltrator* -- the reference's command lets an Infiltrator user through for direct and move-effect side effects (not Transform or Sky Drop); here Leech Seed, Gastro Acid, Worry Seed, Nightmare, Simple Beam and the status subscripts still stop it. -- fixed in 9b7619b5f and fb7bed50a
- **low** *Heal Block's target-side half* -- the reference refuses Heal Pulse on a heal-blocked target and Pollen Puff on a heal-blocked ally. -- fixed in 8033654af
- **medium** *resist berries outside the damage chain* -- subscript_0264 halves the damage after the unhalved value was stored for Counter, Mirror Coat, Metal Burst, Shell Bell and the Endure and Sash clamps, and takes the berry's type from the move rather than the adjusted type (Pixilate and the others, Liquid Voice, Ion Deluge); the reference halves inside the chain (6.9.13) and eats the berry before the hit. -- fixed in 6e136f163
- **low** *the final modifier's order between battlers* -- the reference visits the battlers by raw Speed; FinalDamageModifier takes attacker, target, ally, the same order whenever the attacker is faster. -- fixed in bb7c5c278
- **low** *Minimize doubling by list* -- the reference doubles Body Slam, Stomp, Dragon Rush, Steamroller, Heat Crash, Heavy Slam, Flying Press, Malicious Moonsault and Supercell Slam against a Minimized target and makes them always hit; here only effect 150 doubles. -- fixed in a86d5b3e6
- **low** *Spit Up has no damage roll* -- CalcDamageRaw keeps Gen 4's; the reference rolls. -- fixed in c8dc5d418
- **low** *Future Sight is worked out when used* -- the reference works it out when it lands. Round 5 left it: the landing has to keep the user's party slot, run DamageCalcDefault with the user in or its party stats when gone, and teach subscript 121 the chart. -- fixed in 70ec071a9; a Disguise, Ice Face or Tera Shell is not yet asked at landing
- **low** *Stuff Cheeks makes Recycle and Harvest forget the Berry* -- the subscript's second RemoveItem leaves recycleItem empty. -- fixed in e3121b4a4
- **low** *once-per-battle entry abilities are shared by multi-battle partners* -- onceOnlyEntryAbilityDone is by side and party slot, so the partner's slot 0 blocks the player's. -- fixed in c0465e637
- **low** *a Berry flung at a Cud Chew Pokemon is not kept* -- TryFling runs before the hit is known. -- fixed in 28326b27d
- **low** *Gastro Acid spares only Multitype* -- the games' unsuppressible set applies there too. -- fixed in 361df8069
- **low** *the trainer AI sees Judgment with a Pixie Plate as Normal* -- the three sites that type Judgment for the AI read the plate the old way. -- fixed in 4236a5f56, 18a99f944 (matching) and d407c5c41
- **medium** *Electric types can be paralysed* -- subscript_0031_Paralyze.s has no Electric-type test; its "doesn't affect" block is unreachable after the `GoTo` that ends the block above it. The reference's subscript sends an Electric type there. -- fixed in b48005002
- **medium** *Safeguard still stops a held item's status* -- subscripts 0025 (Flame Orb), 0037 and 0047 (Toxic Orb) test SIDE_CONDITION_SAFEGUARD on the held-item path; the reference comments that out as a Generation V change. -- fixed in 9c5c2aa9d
- **medium** *Protect's odds on a run are Gen 4's* -- sProtectSuccessChance (asm/overlay_12_battle_command.s) halves each time and stops at 1/8; the reference divides by three each time. -- fixed in 5d57ce707 (matching) and 9533136ca
- **low** *Sheer Force and Covert Cloak miss five moves* -- IsSuppressibleSecondaryEffect needs a side-effect flag and a chance above 0, which leaves out Psychic Noise, Anchor Shot, Spirit Shackle, Thousand Waves and Throat Chop; the reference lists them. -- fixed in e7b21ff36
- **low** *Heal Block uses retail's move list* -- the reference's effect list also blocks the draining moves, Dream Eater, Matcha Gotcha, Heal Pulse and Life Dew. -- fixed in a490618c9
- **low** *a substitute does not stop nine imported moves* -- Soak, Magic Powder, Forest's Curse, Trick-or-Treat, Quash, Guard Split, Power Split, Heal Pulse and Decorate: their subscripts have no substitute check, which the reference makes in C before the move. -- fixed in 5b32744ec
- **low** *imported moves missing a piece* -- Surging Strikes can miss its critical hits (effect_script_0368 lacks CRITICAL_STAGE_ALWAYS); Matcha Gotcha does not thaw a frozen user; Mortal Spin only poisons, without Rapid Spin's clearing; Shed Tail makes the decoy and never switches out, and its subscript resets the user's stages; Stuff Cheeks eats its berry at +6 Defense; Coaching does not fail without a partner; Hyperspace Fury does not lift an ally's team guard as Feint now does; Greninja-Ash's Water Shuriken does not always hit three times. -- fixed in 5c8581d86, 443d539d8, 790498bcf, 6caa33c16, 7539469e9, 9c61e8de1 and 6133cddf6; Hyperspace Fury already does what the reference does
- **low** *Comatose reads the reference has and this tree has not* -- Nightmare, the Role Play, Skill Swap, Gastro Acid and Worry Seed block lists, and the sleep bonus to a catch. -- fixed in 9969112f4
- **low** *an Ability Capsule's swap is undone by the next ability reset* -- the capsule writes the ability outright, and UpdateBoxMonAbility, run on evolution and on form changes, works it out again from the personality; the reference keeps a swap bit that its reset honours. The field it uses here already holds the Mint's nature bits. -- fixed in 7bb4ac919
- **low** *the party menu closes after a Potion even with more in the bag* -- the reference's PartyMenu_ItemUseFunc_ReuseItem keeps "Use on which Pokemon?" open after an item that is not an evolution item while more remain. -- fixed in 701ae7403
- **medium** *retail species' level-up learnsets are pret's, not hg-engine's* -- the importer kept pret's learnsets for HeartGold's 493 species apart from konefr's fifteen, while the engine at d0380a487 carries modern ones; by the provenance rule those are the engine layer's values. The visible case: Dunsparce never learns Hyper Drill (hg-engine teaches it at 32), so Dudunsparce, and its passive Three-Segment form, cannot be had. -- fixed in a65853039 (hg-engine's) and ef3b52436 (konefr's fifteen over them); Dunsparce learns Hyper Drill at 32
- **low** *trainer species above 1023* -- trainer_data.c masks a party entry's species with 0x3FF; no trainer holds one today. -- fixed in 166d1b1c0, before the row was written
- **low** *a fresh worktree's first build hangs* -- on a clean checkout make spends half an hour or more re-scanning missing generated files (msg_0729.gmm is an order-only prerequisite of every message file, headers.done of every script); building those targets first, or the ROM target directly, avoids it. -- fixed in af57c1ed5 and 0aa38ff5a

## Found in the fifth round, 2026-09-23

- **medium** *Orichalcum Pulse never turns the sunlight harsh* -- only its Attack boost is written; nothing on entry sets the sun, where the reference has a switch-in step and subscript 487. -- fixed in d4432d1f7
- **medium** *Intimidate ignores the later games' blockers* -- Inner Focus, Oblivious, Own Tempo and Scrappy do not block it and Rattled does not answer it (subscript_0186_Intimidate.s); the reference does not either. -- fixed in 1294f89ef and b32247bd5
- **medium** *Berserk, Anger Shell and Pickpocket ignore Sheer Force* -- their check reads ctx->unk_2174 after command 29 has cleared it. -- fixed in 400565bf9; and three more of Sheer Force's exceptions from Pokemon Central: 84fb2f024, f109afdef and 5ea21921f
- **medium** *Emergency Exit and Wimp Out answer only a move's damage* -- recoil, Life Orb, hazards, weather, status, Leech Seed and Curse do not set them off; Pokemon Central says hazards do. The reference has the same gap. -- fixed in df7e47aa4, d1df82022 and 55f3053d6; the end of a turn is asked once its last effect is over, so a Leftovers heal in between keeps it in; a missed High Jump Kick arms nothing
- **medium** *item-driven forms* -- Genesect's Drive, Silvally's Memory (RKS System reads plates in both trees), Ogerpon's masks and the origin items set no form here. -- fixed in 8a8902879, 38992eaa3, be189899c, cc254f248 and 356311335
- **medium** *items a species keeps* -- the reference's CanItemBeRemovedFromSpecies protects Drives, Memories, masks, the Rusted Sword and Shield, the Griseous Core, Booster Energy and mail from Trick, Knock Off, Fling, Magician and Pickpocket; here only the Griseous Orb and Multitype, and Knock Off still refuses any Multitype holder's item. -- fixed in 1e32790c0
- **medium** *an added species' Dex number is its species id* -- Lillipup prints No. 0508, national 506, and Pecharunt 1041, national 1025 (Pokedex_ConvertToCurrentDexNo); the reference prints national numbers. -- fixed in a73b8aa72
- **low** *three heaps within 1.4 KB of full* -- measured in d69344404: heap 1 (the save) keeps 652 bytes at its fullest, heap 64 408 and heap 69 1,340; any growth there fails. -- closed: the reference enlarges nothing the port has not already enlarged (heap 1 is FULL_SAVE_SIZE under EXPAND_PC_BOXES, heap 3 cut to make room); the margins stand as measured, and the next growth of those heaps is where to look first
- **low** *Solar Blade is not halved outside the sun* -- CalcMoveDamage checks only Solar Beam. -- fixed in 37a5f4132
- **low** *Powder cancels the Fire move before its PP is spent* -- case 17 of ov12_0224B528 runs before the PP is deducted. -- fixed in 13051e6de

## Found in the sixth round, 2026-09-23

- **high** *every imported item showed another file's icon* -- the icon archive is packed by file name, so item_icon_1000 sorted before item_icon_101 and 343 of the 518 imported icons pointed at another member, some at a palette, which hung the bag on an assertion (the DNA Splicers). -- fixed in ab8abb720
- **medium** *a renamed subscript's old .bin is packed into the archive* -- the archive rule packs every .bin in the folder, so a script renumbered or removed leaves its old .bin in the next build and every script after it runs the wrong code; the integration removes them by hand. A test that member N is subscript_N, and a rule that packs only what a .s makes, are wanted. -- fixed in 2da11f15f; test_build_rules holds member N of each battle script archive to script N
- **medium** *the tests count three abilities done by a list alone* -- test_ability_effects' "read by the game" check takes a block list as a reading, and Commander, Gulp Missile and Poison Puppeteer pass that way; whether each really does what Pokemon Central says is to check. -- fixed: Poison Puppeteer (66281a56e) and Gulp Missile (a76cef294) do what Pokemon Central says, a block list no longer counts as a reading (acc7c51ae), and Commander is written in 981ec26bd, after 28f300daa (matching): the pair in a double battle, Tatsugiri hidden and out of every move's reach, Dondozo's five stats up two, neither leaving nor forced out, Tatsugiri back when Dondozo faints (Pokemon Central, Torre di Comando); with what the merge and its review found, 973f915bc, 457646f95 and 6707bba75. PENDING is empty; not yet seen on screen, a row of its own
- **medium** *78 moves the engine leaves unimplemented still do nothing* -- FLAG_UNUSABLE_UNIMPLEMENTED moves keep effect 0 and cannot be learnt or used, Dragon Cheer and Doodle among them; Paolo's rule is that nothing the reference leaves empty stays empty, and import_moves.IMPLEMENTED_HERE records each one done. The seventh round wrote 54 of the 60 that are not Dynamax's (33ce772a8 to f5aa5b4e2, each in IMPLEMENTED_HERE), and the 18 Max moves are Dynamax's, out of scope by SCOPE.md; left are Water, Fire and Grass Pledge (the combination at 150 power, the sea of fire, the swamp and the rainbow, which Court Change would swap too), Ally Switch, Sky Drop and Revival Blessing (a fainted party member picked mid-move, by the player and by the AI), each new controller code in overlay 12. -- fixed in 0431d6c0a and 9752fcd0f, after 45f889314 (matching): the ninth round wrote the last two, Sky Drop (the lift turn and the drop with both Pokemon out of reach, the target held and let go, its failures by weight, substitute, ally and Gravity; the two ways out the merge's review found closed in 3420805a9 and af2e58f64) and Revival Blessing (a fainted party member picked mid-move, by the player in the battle party menu's new mode and by the trainer AI, the first fainted in its party). Both seen in the headless harness: Pidgeot took the opposing Furret into the sky, both hidden, and dropped it the next turn; Heracross refused with 'It won't have any effect.' and Machamp revived. The AI's pick and the send-out in a double battle were not seen, a row of their own. The 18 Max moves are Dynamax's, out of scope by SCOPE.md

## Found in the seventh round, 2026-09-24

What the seventh round's nine branches, their merge, the boot arena's budget
and the landing on newgold found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a
second look; "decision" on Paolo.

- **high** *battles with a Surge user hang* -- Tapu Koko, Lele, Bulu and Fini and Mega Raichu X announce their terrain for ever, on either side: BtlCmd_AbilityPopup cleared abilityActivatedFlag, the Surges' only once-guard, which the reference's command leaves alone. Found by the species check. -- fixed in 6482b58d3
- **high** *the boot arena ran out* -- the main arena starts where overlay 12 ends, and the round's moves moved it 0x1460 on: the file system's table (0x1B80) no longer fitted and all three ROMs stayed on a white screen, the diagnostics build from the eight branches on. The heaps take the arena at boot, and test_heaps measured only heap 3's own room. -- fixed in 24d3d77c5 (the default heap 0xD200 to 0x8000 in both builds; its worst measured use is the communication-error screen's 0x5950) and f6c1b9364 (test_heaps holds each built ROM's arena to the byte); 0x4408 left at the worst pre-size, 0x3008 in the diagnostics build
- **medium** *18 added cries never played* -- Samurott, Guzzlord, Coalossal, Eternatus, Glastrier, Calyrex, Maushold, Arboliva, Garganacl, Klawf, Espathra, Bombirdier, Orthworm, Cetitan, Dondozo, Baxcalibur, Fezandipiti, Archaludon and the forms that share them: bank and wave archive were over the cry player's 24,200-byte heap. -- fixed in 1f5eecc29, the 18 resampled at 7.2-10.4 kHz; ADPCM, as the reference, if the lower rates are heard
- **medium** *savedit's Dex set seen and caught only* -- never the genders seen or the form orders, so an edited save asserted in the Dex on a female-only species and showed Sky Shaymin, Origin Giratina and East Sea Shellos as first forms; saves edited before keep the bad bits. -- fixed in 00172ca04, on the save editor's rewrite
