- **medium** *five moves use the ordinary stats* -- Foul Play (the target's Attack), Body Press (the user's Defense), Psyshock, Psystrike and Secret Sword (the target's Defense) go through CalcMoveDamage as any move; the reference's CalcBaseDamage handles them. -- fixed in 1eaa211e7
- **medium** *Photon Geyser neither ignores the ability nor goes physical* -- and the ability-ignoring path Sunsteel Strike and Moongeist Beam take lacks the reference's two guards, an ability that can be broken and attacker != defender. -- fixed in 3a912ea4a (the two guards) and 78a60e4c9
- **medium** *post-move effects past effect 276 still come with the hit* -- Stone Axe, Ceaseless Edge, Mortal Spin, Jaw Lock, Anchor Shot, Spirit Shackle, Fell Stinger and Scale Shot, which the engine gives after the move; they want the post-move pass the binding moves took, and ov12_02250490's Parental Bond hold-back list. -- fixed in 32186fc4d, bfcf2e45e, 20e208269, a5f09aef5, b7c9aa6fd and e208a89ce, Mean Look out of the Parental Bond hold-back list in 20e208269; with Pokemon Central's corrections on the way: Fell Stinger +3, Scale Shot's two changes independent, Thousand Waves holding every foe it hits and no additional effect (1885b729b), Shield Dust keeping Anchor Shot's and Spirit Shackle's hold off (b8b9bc8f3), Sheer Force powering Stone Axe with no stones (9a978f65d)
- **medium** *a catch with a nickname asserts when the party is full* -- the caught Pokemon nicknamed and sent to Box 30 fails CopyWindowToVram+0x14 under RunTextPrinter; seen in the harness on the diagnostics build, where the comm system is up, as the communication-error screen. To reproduce on melonDS. -- fixed in 769fc5e56: any nickname with experience to give did it, whatever the party; the experience printed into the window the naming screen had taken down. It comes straight after "Gotcha!" now, as from Gen VI and in the reference
- **low** *Chloroblast's recoil* -- taken only when it hits, and Rock Head stops it; Pokemon Central: half the user's maximum HP, hit or miss, whatever the ability. -- closed: the premise was wrong; Pokemon Central (Clorofillaser) and Bulbapedia take the recoil only on a hit and let Rock Head and Magic Guard stop it, as TryRecoil did. What was wrong was Reckless boosting it, fixed in 973e1976f
- **low** *Throat Chop silences on a miss* -- the silence is set in the effect script before the accuracy check. -- fixed in 62bfc2b3a
- **low** *the Fangs' status and flinch are on-hit side effects* -- so Sheer Force neither drops nor boosts them and the Covert Cloak does not stop them. -- fixed in 57f64eab9
- **low** *Magnet Rise's Ground immunity ignores Gravity* -- in CalcTypeEffectiveness. -- fixed in c4ecc9ab8, the Air Balloon with it
- **low** *Stuff Cheeks eats the Berry only when its own condition holds* -- it should eat any held Berry, then raise Defense by two. -- fixed in f41728754
- **low** *Burn Up and Double Shock leave a Normal type* -- RemoveBattlerType writes TYPE_NORMAL where its comment says typeless; the reference's RemoveType writes TYPE_TYPELESS. As Normal it is immune to Ghost and weak to Fighting. -- fixed in 4120d1ceb
- **low** *Order Up raises nothing without Commander* -- its raise depends on the Commander row. -- fixed in 000e5e360, after Commander (981ec26bd)
- **low** *Gems can be flung* -- item_data.csv gives them a fling power of 30; the engine's Fling refuses them. To confirm. -- fixed in b36bf37fb
- **low** *the post-move order after the Life Orb is not the engine's* -- here Parting Shot, Pickpocket, Throat Spray and the Eject Pack come before Emergency Exit, Wimp Out and U-turn; the engine asks Emergency Exit (22.0), then U-turn and Parting Shot (23.0), Pickpocket (24.0), Throat Spray (26.0), the Eject Pack (28.0). -- fixed in 09eac39a7
- **low** *Magician and Pickpocket act in wild battles* -- Pokemon Central (Prestigiatore, Arraffalesto) says they do not; CanStealHeldItem has no check. -- fixed in 9c83c5e49
- **low** *Pickpocket takes nothing after a Red Card* -- the attacker is already dragged out; Pokemon Central (Arraffalesto) says the holder still steals. -- fixed in f2bd2dd9a; and Pickpocket takes nothing from a Sticky Hold user, in 5668f298a
- **low** *Knock Off's line comes after "fainted"* -- the post-move pass runs after the faint; Pokemon Central (Privazione) removes the item first. Message order only. -- closed: the premise was wrong; Pokemon Central (Privazione) has no line putting the removal before the faint, and its Gen V rule, no removal when the user faints straight after the hit (Rough Skin, Aftermath, recoil, Destiny Bond), needs the target's faint first: the engine's order (the faint at 11.1, Knock Off at 15.1) and this one
- **low** *Dragon Tail's target keeps quiet before a Rocky Helmet fells the user* -- Battler_WillBeDraggedOut is asked when the hit is answered, so its Pickpocket, Color Change and Anger Shell are skipped though it stays in (a ponytail comment in overlay_12_0224E4FC.c). -- fixed in 90bae2a3e
- **low** *Conversion, Soak and Magic Powder refuse only Multitype* -- the reference's Soak and Magic Powder refuse RKS System too. -- fixed in e52da5801 and def23a740, Conversion 2 and Camouflage with them; Soak and Magic Powder had refused nothing, not even Multitype. They also fail on a target already of their type now (a7efdcaff), and Magic Powder's line names its target (8f2a16620)
- **low** *a Parental Bond second strike that misses loses what the first held back* -- MOVE_STATUS_MULTI_HIT_DISRUPTED after the loop started; whether this game rolls the second strike's accuracy is to confirm. -- closed in b28c890b5: not a defect; the second strike rolls no accuracy, one check for both strikes (Pokemon Central, Amorefiliale), and the test pins it
- **low** *the ability-changing moves against Commander, Protosynthesis and Zen Mode* -- Pokemon Central's pages disagree (Torre di Comando, Paleoattivazione, Gastroacido, Affannoseme, Stato Zen); the port follows the table. Decision. -- fixed in cef567a68, by each page's latest game version (Paolo): Gastro Acid, Worry Seed and Core Enforcer work on Commander from Scarlet and Violet 2.0.1, Gastro Acid and Core Enforcer on Protosynthesis and Quark Drive from 3.0.0; Zen Mode stays unsuppressable. Entrainment is a row of its own
- **low** *the trainer AI's damage estimate leaves Weather Ball untyped* -- ov10_0221F084 gives it no weather type or doubled power, retail's gap. -- fixed in 9e60af45b
- **low** *a transformed Cramorant neither catches nor spits* -- Pokemon Central says only that a copy cannot spit; changing form here would write the copier's party entry. To confirm. -- closed in 850c910fb: confirmed on Pokemon Central (Inghiottimissile), which is what the port did; only the comment changed
- **low** *Sand Spit writes over the map's weather* -- as Pokemon Central (Sputasabbia) gives no exception; the reference goes through Sand Stream's subscript and refuses. To confirm. -- fixed in fe57dcd2f: from the ninth generation nothing overwrites the map's weather (Terrempesta and the other weather abilities' pages), and the reference refuses too; the weather moves are a row of their own
- **low** *two Surge users entering together both announce the terrain* -- the comment calls it the reference's behaviour. To confirm. -- fixed in 394eb2da5: a Surge onto its own terrain says nothing and renews no turns (the four Surge pages); two different Surges both act, the slower last
- **low** *Burning Jealousy and Alluring Voice see only the stat command's raises* -- statRaised is set by ChangeStatStage alone; a raise a script writes (Belly Drum, Anger Point, Steam Engine) is not seen. -- fixed in 01c075887
- **low** *BtlCmd_TriggerAbilityOnHit is unused* -- no script runs it since subscript 175 took the engine's shape; it is still in the command table. -- closed in 4e2c59009: the premise was wrong; subscript 153, Pursuit striking a Pokemon that switches out, runs it twice, the only way that hit is answered, and the test keeps it in the table
- **low** *Silvally's picture ignores its Memory* -- the type follows the Memory, the picture does not: no Silvally case in the pokegra switch and no form species. Whether the follower follows it is to check. -- closed (Paolo, 2026-09-24): konefr's placeholders stay, and per-Memory pictures are ported only if konefr adds them; no Silvally can be met in New Gold as ported
- **low** *the critical-hit mark may outlive a link battle* -- the link end path never reaches BattleSystem_CheckEvolution, which clears it; whether that path copies the party back is unverified. -- closed in 2ea40d59d: not a defect; a link battle outside the Frontier fights a copy of the party and copies only the Pokedex back, and the test holds the three facts that rests on. The Frontier is a row of its own
- **low** *species with the reference's placeholder picture* -- Iron Crown's Dex picture is Bulbasaur's, seen at the landing: hg-engine ships Bulbasaur's picture for it and 86 other sprite folders (six of them on the female picture only: Mega Magearna, Mega Meowstic's female and the four Ogerpon Terastal masks). None of them can be met in New Gold as ported; art from outside the reference is a decision. -- closed (Paolo, 2026-09-24): konefr's placeholders stay, and art is ported only if konefr adds it
- **low** *a battle script archive is not rebuilt when only a script is deleted* -- the archive depends on the .bin files, so deleting the last .s makes nothing newer; a .narcorder rewritten only when it changes, as a prerequisite, would. -- fixed in dc5f8c405
- **low** *field scripts pack their whole folder* -- scr_seq.narc is still built by the generic rule, so a removed or renumbered script leaves its old .bin and moves every event after it. -- fixed in 9c8427572
- **low** *the .narcorder sorts by name* -- a script past 9999, or one without zero padding, would sort out of number order; test_build_rules would catch it. -- fixed in 9d29b5944
- **low** *the harness sometimes draws white for a whole run* -- 3 runs of about 10 with the same input; test_boot's diagnostics boot failed once in the suite and passed rerun. -- fixed in 0864975e1: the clock; the boot's pre-size is drawn from data that includes the RTC, which the core takes from the host, so an arena short by less than 0x100 booted at some seconds and not at others. test_boot pins the clock and measures the arena left
- **low** *import_dex_metrics.py reports 'updated 729' on a rerun* -- the body styles it puts back; the json does not change. -- fixed in a6fcfa0c7
- **low** *the arena test has gaps* -- test_heaps skips itself when nothing is built and never checks build/soulsilver.us.diag. -- fixed in c377d21fe
- **low** *heap 0 at 0x8000 is not measured everywhere* -- the Pokeathlon (estimated 0x6000), link and Wi-Fi scenes, and an error screen raised inside a Wi-Fi app, were not reached. -- closed: measured in the eighth round on the diagnostics build, an NPC trade, the Union Room, the GTS to its connection screen, the Pokeathlon to its team choice and the Wi-Fi settings: heap 0 never had less than 27,388 bytes in one piece. Round 7's Pokeathlon estimate was wrong, its two allocations being HEAP_ID_POKEATHLON's. What was not reached is estimated from the code, the worst the communication-error screen inside Wii Message Settings at 0x5EC0, which leaves 0x2140 of 0x8000
- **low** *other screens that draw a Pokemon's types* -- the Normal-second-type check was not looked for in the battle's own Pokemon pages. -- fixed in d06624b7d, after 32017edc7 (matching): the Dex's capture page; the summary and the battle's party and summary pages were right
- **low** *the species check's blind spots* -- the Dex's FORMS page was not walked, and the summary's Info page was looked at by hand, Litleo and Girafarig. -- fixed in 3a51b33e5: the walk takes the Dex's DETAILS, 1025 AREA pages and 1823 FORMS entries, and the summary's Info page; a retail species' later forms are a row of its own
- **low** *Sneasler's body style* -- Bulbapedia says tailless, PokeAPI and Pokemon Central tailed; the port keeps Bulbapedia's. To confirm. -- closed in a679d8798: tailless, as 8ae70cd5b gave it; the shape is a search in the games' own Dex, Sneasler is only in Pokemon HOME's, and Bulbapedia's list follows HOME
- **info** *"KYUREM changed Forme!" is shown before the panels change* -- the partner leaves once the line is dismissed, by design since 36a24a778. Decision. -- closed: the port's order stands; no source for the games' order was found (Bulbapedia's DNA Splicers and Kyurem pages, a web search), and footage of a later game would settle it
- **info** *a fled battle does not count for the critical-hit evolution* -- it rests on this audit's row, not on Pokemon Central, which asks three critical hits in one battle and 1 HP at its end. Decision. -- closed in c604b574c: Bulbapedia's Sirfetch'd page asks three critical hits in one battle without escaping or losing, the rule the port follows; the Frontier is a row of its own
- **high** *the Dex's DETAILS goes black on every species past the egg* -- 620a00810 made a species past the egg read no area record, and the area page sized its merged list 1 + (0 - 1) x 4, wrote over the Dex heap's links and hung. It reads the egg's record now, a terminator alone: "Area Unknown" (4b86f732c). -- done
- **high** *the move tutor cannot open its menu* -- MoveTutorChooseMove loaded the 28,214-byte move name bank whole into HEAP_ID_FIELD3, 16,384 bytes; it reads the names one at a time now, and ScrCmd_648 the species names (4f5586f49, test_field_heap). -- done
- **medium** *a search result fails a 34,516-byte allocation* -- the results page loaded the whole height and weight banks into the Dex heap to read one line each; they are read lazily now (edd459123 decomp, f8ae576d1). -- done
- **medium** *a female of the eight split species asks for an empty picture* -- Unfezant, Frillish, Jellicent, Pyroar, Meowstic, Indeedee, Basculegion, Oinkologne: the Dex draws the base's female picture, which New Gold leaves empty; the picture code sends it to the female species (ffd6791d4, a4df02c95). Reachable: a female Espurr or Litleo evolves into the female species. -- done
- **medium** *the Dex's area page has no data for species past the egg* -- "Area Unknown" for all of them, 25 of which are in the wild tables (Applin, Bouffalant, Bunnelby, Dedenne, Espurr, Ferroseed, Fletchling, Foongus, Klink...): the area archives need their records. -- fixed in 5fff64f57 and 264ee2f01: 22 of the 25 show their areas; Galvantula and Escavalier are only in the Bug-Catching Contest, which the page leaves out as retail does, and Kleavor is only in a table nothing reads, a row of its own
- **low** *other whole-bank message loads still in asm* -- bank 237 in ov40_02235E34, ov40_0223EC40, ov70_02238430, ov75_02246F0C; bank 750 in ov14_021F5404, ov68_021E6320, ov68_021E6820, sub_02088894; bank 222 in ov15_021F9CBC, ov31_0225D60C: each needs its heap measured against the bank's new size. -- fixed in db82f827d, df9117db3 and f950c59b1, after 41c38e312, 757fbb15f and 8516837ce (matching): the four Wi-Fi ones open the species names lazily, and test_bank_heaps holds all four; ov40_02235E34 and ov40_0223EC40 are the Vs. Recorder's (overlay 40, msg_0013), not the GTS search, and overlay 75 only destroys the bank it loaded. None of the three apps can be reached in the harness without a Nintendo Wi-Fi Connection ID. The eighth round measured the others on the diagnostics build and fixed the tight ones, the marts (2e31e32fb, matching, and f47b5fb96: 10,572 bytes left, 90,692 now), the Move Relearner (08623a6b5 and f4e54a428, where the load failed) and the summary (6d400fb7c and 937a41c12: 7,076 left, 35,208 now); the bag (64,752 left) and the PC's move popup fit as they are
- **medium** *the Pokemon icon archive packs its whole folder* -- the main tree held 733 stale icon files from an older numbering and its ROM differed from the verified one until they and the archive were deleted; it needs the rule the battle scripts got (2da11f15f on r7-extra1, landed as 2da11f15f). -- fixed in 5a8050b83

## Found in the eighth round, 2026-09-24

What the eighth round's nine branches, their merge and the merge's review
found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look; "decision" on
Paolo.

- **high** *the Move Relearner cannot open* -- it loaded the 28,214-byte move name bank whole into heap 66 with 9,496 bytes left, and the game showed the communication error and reset; seen headlessly in Blackthorn with a Heart Scale. The reference doubles the relearner's heap instead (0x24000 to 0x48000). -- fixed in 08623a6b5 (matching) and f4e54a428, the names read one at a time; at least 30,200 bytes left
- **medium** *a Pokemon Commander holds shuts the Eject Packs with a switch that never comes* -- the merge put the species branch's post-move order, where an Emergency Exit, a Wimp Out or a pending U-turn keeps the move's Eject Packs from acting, beside the battle branch's Commander, which refuses those switches only in the scripts. -- fixed in 973f915bc: Battler_Retreats and PivotSwitchPending ask Battler_HeldByCommander; a held wild Pokemon still flees
- **low** *a held Pokemon's own Eject Button or Eject Pack* -- CheckSwitchItemOnHit and CheckEjectPack answered for a Pokemon Commander holds; the script refused the switch, but the move's switch-item turn was spent and the Eject Packs shut for every other holder. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 6707bba75; a Red Card it holds still drags the attacker out
- **low** *Ally Switch by a Dondozo with a Tatsugiri in its mouth* -- the swap moved the Commander state with the two Pokemon, but each place keeps its sprite's hidden flag, so the Dondozo vanished and the Tatsugiri showed. No Dondozo can learn the move in this tree. -- fixed in 457646f95: effect script 443 swaps the hidden places, as for an ally in the air
- **low** *the unread-effects ratchet stood above its table* -- test_move_effects' STILL_UNREAD said 37 with 33 entries in UNREAD_HERE after the merge. -- fixed in fc43fcdc5
- **low** *the boot arena's figures in this audit go stale* -- "the boot arena ran out" gives 0x3008 for the diagnostics build; at the round's base both diagnostics builds left 0x2f48, and each round moves them. -- closed: test_heaps holds each build's arena to the byte and test_boot measures it at boot; at fc43fcdc5 HeartGold and SoulSilver leave 0x3068, the diagnostics builds 0x1c28
- **medium** *Matcha Gotcha drains only when its burn rolls* -- effect script 352 sets its side effect without ON_HIT, so ov12_02250490 rolls the 20% before subscript 330 runs, and 330 rolls the burn again: a drain on 20% of hits, a burn on 4%, and Sheer Force and the Covert Cloak drop the drain with the burn. The games drain on every hit and burn one time in five; the reference's script is the same. -- fixed in 80128eaed
- **medium** *Grassy Terrain heals nobody at the end of the turn* -- the reference runs subscript 346 from ServerFieldConditionCheck.c; the port has neither the step nor the subscript. When it lands, a Tatsugiri in a Dondozo's mouth is not healed (Torre di Comando). -- fixed in 939c55fba and 25d4e92a6: a sixteenth at the end of every turn for a grounded Pokemon, the terrain's last turn too (Campo Erboso), and not for one out of reach, under Heal Block or in a Dondozo's mouth
- **medium** *Ghost types can be trapped* -- CantEscape and BattlerCanSwitch hold them with Mean Look, the binding moves, Jaw Lock, Anchor Shot and Spirit Shackle, Shadow Tag and Arena Trap; only Fairy Lock spares them, where from Gen VI a Ghost always flees and switches. To confirm on Pokemon Central. -- fixed in 014f43e95: a Ghost-type switches and flees past every hold but Commander's and, since 0431d6c0a, Sky Drop's, and Mean Look, Block and Spider Web fail on it (Pokemon Central, Spettro and Malosguardo; Bulbapedia's Ingrain page)
- **medium** *an item Magician or Pickpocket takes in a trainer battle may stay taken* -- they have no guard like retail Thief's (BtlCmd_TryStealItem), and Pokemon Central gives such items back at the battle's end; whether the port does is unchecked. To confirm. -- fixed in 9c3290462: most came back already through GiveBackHeldItems; what stayed was whatever a Pokemon that began with a Berry took after eating it, and a Berry a trainer's Magician or Pickpocket took from the player was lost for good (Furto, Prestigiatore, Arraffalesto)
- **medium** *a Frontier command counts types in 18 slots* -- FrtCmd_103 case 18 (asm/overlay_80_0222F608.s) counts the next opponents' types in a stack array of 18; a Fairy type writes one past it, into the caller's saved r4, and is never the most common. Needs FrtCmd_103 decompiled and the array sized by NUMBER_OF_MON_TYPES. -- fixed in 9484b53ba (matching) and 89724037e: the array and both loops sized by NUMBER_OF_MON_TYPES; test_factory_type_hint runs case 18 under AddressSanitizer
- **medium** *Kleavor's only wild slot is in a table the contest never reads* -- New Gold's land table for MAP_NATIONAL_PARK_BUG_CATCHING_CONTEST (D22R0102) has Kleavor, Galvantula and Escavalier, while the contest draws from mushi_encount; a Scyther with a Black Augurite still evolves into Kleavor. -- closed: konefr's own data, as his ROM has it. His e9950130e (8 Sep) filled the D22R0102 land table, Kleavor at 1% at every hour; eleven days later ff4c57ff5 added scripts/patch_bug_contest.py, which rewrites mushi_encount's table 0, Butterfree to Escavalier with no Kleavor, because the contest reads that file and not the wild encounter archive, and 150349a62 runs it from his Makefile, so his ROM never reads the D22R0102 species either. This tree's mushi_encount.csv matches that table exactly (test_bug_contest pins it) and encounter_check.c takes contest species only from it. Kleavor so has no source in New Gold or in his ROM, no Black Augurite being placed: his to decide, not the port's
- **low** *the Utility Umbrella reaches only Weather Ball, Hydro Steam and Orichalcum Pulse* -- the rain's and the sun's damage for a holder and Solar Beam's charge read the field without it; the reference is the same. -- fixed in 6e50c13c1: everything Pokemon Central's Superombrello page lists, the Fire and Water damage, the charge turns and Solar Beam's halving, the accuracy of Thunder, Hurricane and the Storms, the healing moves, the weather abilities, Leaf Guard and the forms; Protosynthesis left alone, as Paleoattivazione says
- **low** *a Parental Bond first strike that fells its user drops what it held back* -- after a Rocky Helmet or Rough Skin, Smack Down's fall and the terrain Steel Roller and Ice Spinner end are lost; ov12_0224CF14 gives them back only when sleep stopped the move. -- closed in 2386a4d4e: not a defect; Pokemon Central (Abbattimento, Vortighiaccio) has a fainted user bring nothing down and end no terrain, so giving the held-back effect back only after sleep is right, and the comment says so. A single strike does the opposite, a row of its own
- **low** *Explosion's user stays in when every target fainted before it moved* -- from Gen V it faints anyway (Pokemon Central, Esplosione); the no-target check (ov12_0224B528) runs before the script. -- fixed in a40a886d7
- **low** *Natural Gift at a fainted target keeps its Berry* -- Pokemon Central (Dononaturale) spends it; the no-target check stops the move first. -- fixed in c97826978
- **low** *Emergency Exit and Wimp Out after a Red Card* -- Bulbapedia's Red Card page says they do not act when the card was used on the same move; here they are asked after it. To confirm. -- fixed in c9f8ce9e0
- **low** *a switch item that answers when nobody can come in* -- CheckSwitchItemOnHit and CheckEjectPack answer before subscript SWITCH_OUT_ITEM finds whether anyone can come in; with nobody, the switch is refused as a held Pokemon's was before 6707bba75, and what that costs the move's other holders is unchecked. Older than the round; the merge's review left it. -- fixed in 108d8226b
- **low** *the weather moves write over the map's weather* -- Rain Dance, Sunny Day, Sandstorm and Hail (effect scripts 136, 137, 115, 164) never ask FIELD_CONDITION_OVERWORLD_WEATHER_ANY, where Terrempesta says a move no longer can from the ninth generation. The reference does not refuse either. -- fixed in e90e1b7bc
- **low** *Spectral Thief's rise is not passed to a Mirror Herb* -- its case of BtlCmd_SetMoveConditionFlag writes the stages without RecordMirrorHerbStages. -- fixed in 76ff6fc93
- **low** *Entrainment and Role Play against Commander and the paradox abilities* -- Torre di Comando and Paleoattivazione say Entrainment fails on Commander, Protosynthesis and Quark Drive, and a Commander Tatsugiri cannot Role Play; Saltamicizia's list and the port's table let Entrainment through, and Role Play asks only the target's ability. No page gives a version. Decision. -- closed in ce982eb41: by the latest versions' rule, as for cef567a68: from Scarlet and Violet 2.0.1 Worry Seed, Gastro Acid and Mummy write over Commander and Worry Seed the paradox abilities, which is Saltamicizia's list, the three refused on the user's side only; Role Play already refuses a Commander user and copies none of the three. Only the note changed
- **low** *the trainer AI aims moves at a Tatsugiri in a Dondozo's mouth* -- the moves fail. -- fixed in b04bcac2d
- **low** *Commander, Ally Switch and the Pledges not seen running* -- Commander's hiding, Ally Switch's redraw (the form-change mosaic, no animation of its own) and the Pledges' lines want a double battle on screen; whether Protect's and the Metronome item's counters pause while Tatsugiri is in the mouth (Torre di Comando) was not checked. -- closed in cbf2cc610: the counters pause in the mouth, as Torre di Comando says, and no code changed; test_commander runs the real BtlCmd_TryProtection and ov12_022565E0 around the mouth and lists every writer of the four count fields. All three were seen in the ninth round in a trainer double battle in Azalea Gym, in melonDS on a display of its own (2ce7383bd, tools/newgold/devkit/diag/nested.py): the Pledges combined, the sea of fire and its damage at the turn's end; Ally Switch's mosaic, the swap and its line; Tatsugiri swallowed and hidden with its health box kept, Dondozo's stats up, only Dondozo asked for a move, a Mach Punch at Tatsugiri missing. Hypnosis's animation, played after Ally Switch's line, is fixed in 366209937
- **low** *a Galarian Farfetch'd can evolve in a Battle Frontier battle* -- Frontier battles reach BSTATE_EVOLUTION_INIT and TryCriticalHit marks it; the scene plays on the Frontier's copy and sub_02052444 copies the Dex back, so Sirfetch'd is probably registered caught without being owned. Retail never evolves there, having no experience. Skipping the mark in BATTLE_TYPE_NO_EXP battles would do; the rule for Frontier and link battles is to confirm. -- fixed in 89b1fea28: no critical-hit mark in BATTLE_TYPE_NO_EXP battles, link and Frontier, after which retail evolves nothing
- **low** *the battle party menu's contest icons are one type short* -- ov08_02221328 picks the icon as contest type + 0x12, retail's type count, so Cool shows the Fairy icon; whether that mode (ov08 byte 0x2077) is reachable in HeartGold is to check. -- fixed in a775806d5 (matching) and deda9a744: contest type + NUMBER_OF_MON_TYPES, as the Move Relearner has it; the mode cannot be reached in HeartGold, the high nibble of byte 0x2077 (Platinum's hasVisitedContestHall) being cleared as the menu opens and never set
- **low** *capture experience in the catching tutorial* -- Lyra's Marill is seen gaining experience before the fade now, as it did before on a black screen; CalcExpGain and the reference's BATTLE_TYPE_NO_EXPERIENCE leave the tutorial in, and leaving BATTLE_TYPE_TUTORIAL out is one line. Decision. -- fixed in 15f7ac9a2: the demonstration's party is the scene's; the reference pays it, not copied
- **low** *the caught Pokemon's HP box stays up through its capture experience* -- top left, until the Dex page. To confirm. -- fixed in 9f6bb064b: the box slides out as the faint's does; seen on the diagnostics build, gone when the experience line starts
- **low** *Life Dew is not taken by an ally's Water Absorb, Dry Skin or Storm Drain* -- Pokemon Central (Goccia Vitale) lets them answer it in place of the heal. -- fixed in 8a25bf6bc
- **low** *Body Press reads the user's own Defense under Wonder Room* -- fixed in 401eb3d37: the Defense value with the Sp. Def stage (Schiacciacorpo)
- **low** *the trainer AI has no rule for Ally Switch or the Pledges* -- in a single battle it can pick Ally Switch and fail; default movesets now give it to a trainer's Kadabra or Alakazam from level 15. -- fixed in 9dbc0fb0d: Ally Switch scores as unusable with no ally beside its user; the Pledges are damaging moves, which the scripts' damage rules already read
- **low** *a sprite redrawn in place drops the Illusion* -- RestoreSprite (the emit ov12_02263F8C) and CheckIgnorableAbility's Illusion handling do not draw the disguise, so a disguised Pokemon behind a substitute shows as itself when the doll goes, and Ally Switch redraws with RestoreSprite then. -- fixed in 817145193 (matching) and 63f75ac2d: the substitute's swap and RestoreSprite send one packet that draws the disguise; CheckIgnorableAbility draws nothing and needed nothing
- **low** *Ice Face reads the move table's category* -- not BattleMoveCategory, so a Photon Geyser or Shell Side Arm that goes physical is special to it. -- fixed in 2c524a406
- **low** *Follow Me redirects a move aimed at its user's own ally* -- the generic target branch of ov12_022506D4; the later games do not. -- closed in 5b5f217ce: not a defect; Bulbapedia's Follow Me and Rage Powder pages draw a move aimed at an ally too, unless it cannot target a foe (Acupressure, Helping Hand), and retail and the reference both do; a comment and a test line
- **low** *Stuff Cheeks, Bug Bite and Teatime eat some Berries for nothing* -- TryEatOpponentBerry has no case for the Kee, Maranga, Jaboca, Rowap, Enigma, Custap and Lansat Berries or the type-resist Berries. -- fixed in abc86d0da and df5daa966: the Roseli, Kee and Maranga Berries are Berries to every battle check, and an eaten Enigma, Kee or Maranga feeds its eater; the rest of the premise was wrong, Lansat having its case and the Jaboca, Rowap, Custap and type-resist Berries rightly giving their eater nothing (Baccajaba, Coleomorso)
- **low** *a make with nothing changed still links and packs the ROM* -- the .PHONY archives a/0/7/5, a/1/3/3 and a/2/5/2 and the mwasmarm patch step rerun the link, compression, makerom and fixrom every time; older than the round. -- fixed in 97355e9a8: 3 s against 43 s, nothing linked or packed, and test_build_rules reads make's database; the mwasmarm step still runs and writes nothing
- **low** *the Union Room's heaps are close* -- after entering and walking around, heap 31 had 0x380 bytes left in one piece and heap 87 0x1C50; not compared with retail. To confirm. -- closed: measured in the ninth round, and compared with retail by the code. Both heaps hold only fixed-size structures from code the port never changed (asm/unk_02058AEC.s and asm/unk_0205AC88.s): heap 31 is 0xA80 bytes and 0x380 is what its 0x190 and 0x4E8 leave after the heads, heap 87 is 10,000 and 0x1C50 what the chat log leaves, so retail leaves the same bytes. No bank is loaded whole into either. In the harness the room, its chat and its bag failed no allocation, the chat's heaps 34 and 35 with 24,072 and 54,852 bytes left; other players' lines, which need a second DS, were not reached
- **low** *the FORMS walk sees one form of a retail species* -- savedit marks only the first form seen, so Unown's and the other retail Dex forms past the first were not walked on FORMS. -- fixed in e5f43b51a and b03b099dc: savedit writes a species' whole seen-form order; the walk took 13 AREA pages and 62 FORMS entries, Unown's 28 among them, with no failure
- **low** *jsonproc sorts object keys* -- a template that loops over a json object takes its members in key order, mon_1000 before mon_100; only zukan_enc.json.txt does today, held by zero-padded keys and a test. -- closed in 6de73ee5c: explained and guarded, not changed; nlohmann's ordered_json crashed inja on the first template, and test_archive_rules fails a template that loops over an object unless it is listed with what keeps its order, zukan_enc.json.txt alone today
- **info** *the other harness tools run at the real clock* -- diag/battle.py, smoke.py's routes and walks and diag/core.py, so the RNG and the time of day follow the host's second and one replay can differ from the next; they can pass `clock:` now. -- fixed in 762302bee: they run at a pinned second unless the actions name a clock
- **info** *test_boot boots only HeartGold's diagnostics ROM* -- SoulSilver's is held by test_heaps and was booted by hand. -- fixed in 5b4d3b451
- **info** *the reference's Magic Powder also sets magicPowderFlag* -- what it feeds is unchecked; it may be Terastallization's alone. -- closed in 31ebc17db: its only reader is the reference's end-of-turn step that writes a Roost user's types back, and the port's Roost writes no type, so there is nothing to feed; the comment says so
- **info** *items taken from wild Pokemon* -- from Gen IX they go to the Bag (Pokemon Central, Arraffalesto); here the taker keeps them. Decision. -- fixed in 9c3290462 and d8435ac78: the port already bagged them (7c0dd508e) but after a Berry, where the taker kept them too; and a wild Pokemon caught keeps the item taken from it, the Bag getting no copy (Arraffalesto, Furto)
- **info** *a Pledge combination can outlast its turn after Ally Switch* -- if the marked Pledge misses, pledgeCombination stays set for the turn; only a second Pledge in it (Instruct) would see it. -- fixed in 7f93b0eaf
- **info** *Commander's line has no source but Pokemon Showdown* -- neither Bulbapedia nor Pokemon Central quotes it; the port uses Showdown's transcription of the games'. -- fixed in cf53e4eb3: Scarlet and Violet's own English text (the sv-text dump, common_eng.txt 8219-8222), the wild and opposing lines naming the Dondozo bare
- **info** *the Dex's area page follows retail's rules* -- no swarms, Safari Zone or Bug-Catching Contest, so a contest-only species shows "Area Unknown", as retail's Scyther and Pinsir do. Decision. -- closed: the rules are retail's. dex_areas.py rebuilds retail's zukan_enc byte for byte, and switching each rule off in turn against pret's data changes retail's records (23 with swarms, 21 with the contest, 76 with the Safari Zone, 2 with the contest map's land table). New Gold's 'Area Unknown' species are of retail's kinds: contest-only (Pinsir, Galvantula, Escavalier and six more), swarm-only (seven) and Safari-only (Lapras). Showing more than retail does would be Paolo's to ask for
- **info** *scene.py lives in the round's scratch* -- it drives the harness from the command line, presses, touches, screenshots and heap low-water readings, and reached the mart, the relearner, the summary, the bag, the PC, trades, the Union Room, the GTS and the Pokeathlon; it could go into tools/newgold/devkit/diag. Decision. -- fixed in b243595ef: tools/newgold/devkit/diag/scene.py, in the devkit's README

## Found in the ninth round, 2026-09-24

What the ninth round's nine branches, their merge, the merge's review and the
gym replays found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look;
"decision" on Paolo.

- **high** *every held item read another item's record in battle* -- c708be76a (2026-09-22) took ItemData's last two padding bytes out and left it 34 bytes, while the item archive keeps its records 36 apart and the battle steps through the loaded table by the struct's size: record N was read 2N bytes early, its hold effect, parameter, Fling and Natural Gift another's. Falkner's Delibird was badly poisoned by its Focus Sash, whose hold-effect byte fell on the Blue Scarf's price, 100, the Toxic Orb's effect; in the eighth round's replay the same Sash failed against a full-health Thunder. Found replaying Falkner; newgold's tip has it. -- fixed in fd3162ac5: the two bytes back after the union and a host test on sizeof(ItemData) == 36; "Shuppet hung on using its Focus Sash" and the Sitrus Berry's and Black Sludge's lines seen in the gym logs, the battle heap now loading an item table of 0x17778 bytes (0x1629C before)
- **high** *the battle party menu's entry moved on r9-battle* -- 9dbc0fb0d (on r9-battle; landed fixed as 9dbc0fb0d) defined AllySwitchHasNoAlly ahead of ov10_0221BE20 and moved that routine 0x4C from the start of overlay 10; overlay 12's three calls to the battle party menu's entry, disassembled as ov10_0221BE20 at ov08_0221BE20's address, then landed 0x4C inside it, and the party menu in battle broke on that branch. Found at the merge, moves-types' 45f889314 (landed as 45f889314) coming out three bl bytes off. -- fixed in 9dbc0fb0d, the helper defined after the routine; 45f889314 calls ov08_0221BE20 by its name
- **medium** *Pickup and Honey Gather never kept what they found* -- BtlCmd_GenerateEndOfBattleItem runs in the win script and GiveBackHeldItems, after it, wrote over the find: wiped after a trainer battle, bagged after a wild one. The reference's Battle_End hook has the same order. -- fixed in c966f119d: the items go back once per battle, before Pickup looks for empty hands (Raccolta)
- **medium** *Lightning Rod and Storm Drain only redirect* -- an Electric or Water move aimed at the holder still damages it and raises nothing, where from Gen V it is absorbed with a Sp. Atk raise; the reference has BATTLE_SUBSCRIPT_HANDLE_LIGHTNING_ROD_RAISE_SPATK. -- fixed in 08c24a1ce (Parafulmine, Acquascolo): an Electric or Water move aimed at the holder, status moves included, is swallowed and raises its Sp. Atk (subscript 472); seen in the headless harness, a wild Pincurchin's Lightning Rod raising its Sp. Atk at each of six Electro Balls, at 20/20 HP throughout. The move is drawn before its PP is spent since 13d7833dd, and the AI script knows the two since b46b1f80b
- **low** *Sky Drop's hold had two ways out* -- TryRetreatAbilityOutsideMove let a lifted Pokemon with Emergency Exit or Wimp Out leave at the turn's end, and a wild one's Run asked only Bind and Mean Look. On r9-moves-types alone as well; found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 3420805a9 and af2e58f64: the end-of-turn ask passes over whatever Battler_KeptOnField holds, Commander's pair too, which Battler_Retreats still lets flee in the wild, where HGSS has no double battle; a held wild Pokemon's Run gets the can't-escape line (Cadutalibera)
- **low** *Ally Switch played a borrowed animation after its line* -- Hypnosis's rings and Zs on the user, twice in melonDS: UseMove's subscript 76 plays the move's animation when effect script 443 has played none. -- fixed in 366209937, seen fixed
- **low** *the diagnostics builds have 0x1008 of the main arena left* -- less than the round's overlay-12 growth, 0xC20, and far less than the 0x2300 of shares it handed out; the plain builds have 0x2428. Not a defect at the tip, but a round of the same size would leave about 0x3E8, and one with such shares a white screen. The next round's shares are to be sized from the diagnostics builds, and every agent to rebuild them and report their margin. -- closed: the tenth round sized its shares from the diagnostics builds, and every branch rebuilt them and reported its growth there; the round took 0x6C0 of the diagnostics builds' room and 0x5A0 of the plain builds', which leaves 0x948 and 0x1E88 at b46b1f80b (test_heaps). What 0x948 means for the next round is a row of the tenth round
- **low** *Snipe Shot is redirected* -- by Follow Me, Rage Powder, Lightning Rod and Storm Drain: BattlerIgnoresRedirection asks only Propeller Tail and Stalwart; the reference's target branch leaves MOVE_SNIPE_SHOT out, and Pokemon Central's Sonoqui page lists it. -- fixed in ae630f98c (Sonoqui)
- **low** *Ally Switch's wild and opposing lines name the pair's side twice* -- "The wild X and the wild Y switched places!"; Scarlet and Violet's own text (sv-text common_eng.txt 7591-7594) names the ally bare, as Commander's line now does. -- fixed in a187e9fff: the two rows name the ally bare, as Scarlet and Violet's text; not seen, no opponent here knowing Ally Switch
- **low** *the trainer AI picks Mean Look, Block, Spider Web and Octolock against a Ghost-type* -- its move scripts are still a byte blob (ov10_02220AAC), with no rule that these fail on one now. -- fixed in 7fab3ef10: flag 0 marks Mean Look, Block, Spider Web and Octolock down (MOVE_EFFECT_PREVENT_ESCAPE, MOVE_EFFECT_OCTOLOCK) against a Ghost-type or a target already held; ai_ghost_not_held.json, Falkner's Hoothoot using Leer on a Gengar where it used Mean Look before
- **low** *the Vs. Recorder's search by Pokemon uses retail's letter groups* -- ov40_02245CD4 and ov40_02245E44 keep retail's bounds (0, 87, 136, 191, 235, 303, 360, 457, 487, 493) in the Dex's alphabetical list, which has 1025 species: YZ shows Lickitung to Linoone, and nothing after Linoone can be picked. From this tree's list they are 0, 186, 313, 432, 512, 629, 741, 947, 1005, 1025. Wi-Fi only; a data change in overlay 40's asm, or the table generated. -- fixed in dc8d9641e (matching) and bebd18dd7: the nine groups follow this Dex's alphabetical list, which a host test checks; online only, not seen
- **low** *what jsonproc builds does not depend on jsonproc* -- no output lists $(JSONPROC), the gap f9de102b7 closed for o2narc, so a change to its output leaves old archives until they are deleted by hand. -- fixed in 54b2a9d10: all 17 kinds of jsonproc output depend on it; the same gap for the other tools is a row of the tenth round
- **low** *the Battle Hall's type ranks have no Fairy* -- ov80_022310C4 keeps 18 per-type rank slots, ??? among them (loops bounded by 0x12 near 0x02231324 and 0x02231344); it seems to index its own categories, not a type, so it should not overflow. Whether Fairy should be one is a design question. Not traced. To confirm. -- fixed in 24ef4532a, as Paolo decided on 2026-10-02: Fairy is the Hall's eighteenth category, in the rank slot retail left unused, so the save keeps its layout and an old save's Fairy rank starts at 1, and ov80_022319B0's reset counts eighteen; the type board's summary cell is one cell wide to make room for a Fairy cell, whose pink takes the palette index ???'s strip gave up, and the category's trainers are a Lass, a Pokefan, a Beauty and a Lady (retail's unused row had an Idol, a Clown, a Waiter and a Waitress). The steps before it are matching C, the ROMs byte-identical (6b7708db2, 4dddd8b85), and the frontier's graphics archive a/1/8/3 is built from its members (d1fa990e2). battle_hall_fairy_rank.json (312b93def) picks Fairy on the board and fights its opponent; since a97054a95 (the merge's review) its RNG fields a Cleffa, which only a Fairy pick brings, and it checks the win
- **low** *held items keyed on a species miss its forms* -- the Leek asks SPECIES_FARFETCHD, not Galarian Farfetch'd (1125); the Thick Club Cubone and Marowak, not Alolan Marowak; the Light Ball SPECIES_PIKACHU, not the Pikachu forms. hg-engine keeps the base species with a form number; sFormBaseSpecies maps a form to its base. None of these forms can be met in New Gold as ported. -- fixed in e8133823b; none of these forms can be met in New Gold, so by host tests only
- **low** *a pure Flying-type that uses Roost is typeless* -- Gen IV's rule; from Gen V it is Normal for the turn (Trespolo), as the reference's HandleRoost sets. Here Ghost moves hit it and Fighting is neutral. -- fixed in fd22433d9
- **low** *a trainer's Thief and Covet take nothing from the player* -- retail's side guard in BtlCmd_TryStealItem, which test_stealing_still_is pins; from Gen V they take the item and it comes back at the end (Furto), which NoteHeldItemTaken and GiveBackHeldItems make safe now. Decision. -- fixed in 81dfc806e (Furto): a trainer's Thief and Covet take the player's item, which comes back at the battle's end; a wild Pokemon still takes nothing, and test_stealing_still_is is test_only_a_wild_pokemon_steals_nothing now. Not seen running
- **low** *a Trick onto a wild Pokemon then caught duplicates the item* -- the caught Pokemon keeps the player's item while the player's Pokemon gets its own back, and the wild one's goes to the bag. Older than the round; the reference does the same. -- fixed in b51f81591 (Raggiro, Rapidscambio): the caught Pokemon keeps the item it was handed, the player's keeps what it got, and nothing is copied; the same for an item a foe's Magician or Pickpocket took. Host tests
- **low** *the catching demonstration's first turn* -- Marill's first move on the Lv. 2 Rattata, with accuracy and the critical hit forced; if New Gold's numbers ever let it knock the Rattata out, the tutorial would stop and retail's CalcExpGain pay experience. Estimated at 9-10 damage against about 13 HP; not run. To confirm. -- closed in bdc1ffdad: the first move cannot knock the Rattata out, since with the engine's learnset the Lv. 5 Marill's first move is Tail Whip, a status move; seen through gDiagForceTutorial (b845db236), the Rattata's HP bar full throughout. Its trainer's line after it is a row of the tenth round
- **low** *a combined Pledge says the sea of fire after its target faints* -- "The opposing Spinarak fainted!" came before "A sea of fire enveloped the opposing team!": the condition is set in the post-move additional-effects step (MOVE_EFFECT_PLEDGE), where Showdown sets it with the hit. To confirm against the games. -- fixed in 0b389990a: the condition is laid with the hit, as Showdown's gen-9 moveHit (Pokemon Central says nothing); a host test, not replayed on screen
- **low** *a single strike's Smack Down and terrain end outlast a user the hit's answer fells* -- ov12_02250490 does them with the hit, before Rough Skin, Iron Barbs, a Rocky Helmet or a Jaboca Berry answer it; Pokemon Central (Abbattimento, Vortighiaccio) has a fainted user bring nothing down and end no terrain, and only Parental Bond's first strike does so here. The engine does Smack Down at post-move step 15.4 and the terrain at 25.0. -- fixed in f03d60a33: the fall and the terrain's end wait for the post-move steps (step 3 and step 12), as the engine's 15.4 and 25.0; host tests. Static and Flame Body now act while Misty Terrain is still up (Vortighiaccio); Smack Down ends Telekinesis too since 9aa7141ea
- **low** *the Metronome item counts a spread move once per target* -- ov12_022565E0 runs again for every later target, so Earthquake's second target in a double battle takes one more use's boost; retail's controller does the same. To confirm (Metronomo, the item). -- fixed in 3f58cbbe2 and 489b74905 (Plessimetro): a spread move counts once if it hits a target, and keeps that count past a last target that avoided it. A failed or absorbed use is a row of the tenth round
- **low** *Pressure and called moves* -- the calling move's Pressure is reckoned on its own range, for Metronome its user, so a called move aimed at a Pressure holder costs no extra PP. To confirm (Pressione). -- fixed in 230ff4fb2: a calling move pays Pressure for the move it calls and where it goes, as Showdown's gen-9 code; since 13d7833dd a chosen move is drawn by Lightning Rod or Storm Drain before its PP is spent, the engine's order, so both pay at the same Pokemon
- **low** *a frozen target is thawed only by Fire moves* -- controller case 6; Spruzzate says Matcha Gotcha thaws it, and Scald, Steam Eruption, Scorching Sands and Hydro Steam likely do too. To confirm. -- fixed in cb929abbe: Scald, Steam Eruption, Scorching Sands, Hydro Steam and Matcha Gotcha thaw their target (Congelamento, Spruzzate, Showdown for Hydro Steam); and b822ac077 thaws a user of Pyro Ball, Scorching Sands, Hydro Steam or, a Fire type, Burn Up
- **low** *drain moves round the half down* -- DamageDivide; Spruzzate says up. To confirm for the other drain moves. -- fixed in 5aa29ea74: every drain to the nearest, a half up (Spruzzate, Showdown's gen-9 spreadDamage); the Big Root's share as it was
- **low** *the Kee and Maranga Berries' records* -- the Items pocket, Natural Gift type 31, Fling 30 with no effect, where Baccalighia gives Fling 10 raising Defense; the reference's data. -- fixed in 3633e55ea and b0f34eacf (Baccalighia, Baccapane): the Berries pocket, Natural Gift Fairy and Dark at 100, Fling 10 raising Defense or Sp. Def, and the Ganlon and Apicot Berries' pluck effects in place of TryEatOpponentBerry's two cases; nothing in New Gold gives either Berry
- **low** *Bug Bite and Pluck withhold a Berry's effect from a Klutz or Embargo eater* -- TryEatOpponentBerry; from Gen V they give it anyway (Coleomorso). -- fixed in 6ecec8f6d (Coleomorso, Spennata)
- **low** *Opportunist does not see a rise a script writes* -- Belly Drum, a Starf Berry, Anger Point, Spectral Thief; the Mirror Herb does now. -- fixed in c941ddd3b (Scrocco): Opportunist and the Mirror Herb share one record of the rises
- **low** *the Mirror Herb does not copy Dragon Cheer's critical-hit rise* -- Pokemon Central names it. -- fixed in 41523b7b7 (Foglia carbone, Scrocco, Grido del Drago): the Mirror Herb and Opportunist copy a foe's Dragon Cheer; a double battle, not played. Found decompiling it (26ea432a9). -- fixed in fce5bd3ad, through BattleMoveTbl

Retail's slip and the engine's gap, kept by the matching C and commented in trainer_ai_script.c; for the smarter-AI work after the port. -- fixed in ef67aea2b: word 0049 asks Dry Skin, and the flag-0 block sends Earth Eater to the Ground check, Well-Baked Body to the Fire one and Sap Sipper to a new Grass one; ai_earth_eater.json, Falkner's Hoothoot using Leer on an Orthworm where it used Mud-Slap before
- **low** *the AI script's 'moves last' check compares the held item with 107* -- words 0780 and 0789: 107 is HOLD_EFFECT_SPEED_DOWN (Lagging Tail, Full Incense) as a hold effect, the Shiny Stone as an item, so the check never holds. Retail. -- closed with a reason: retail AI, kept for the AI design after the port. The fix: load the hold effect (AI_LOAD_HELD_ITEM_EFFECT) at words 0780 and 0789 and compare it with HOLD_EFFECT_SPEED_DOWN, through a jump to an appended block so no word moves
- **low** *the AI's Guard Spec reads the enemy side's Mist* -- ov10_022206B0 reads fieldSideConditionFlags[1] whatever side the battler is on: wrong for an AI-controlled Pokemon on the player's side. Retail. -- closed with a reason: retail AI, kept for the AI design after the port. The fix: read fieldSideConditionFlags[BattleSystem_GetFieldSide(battleSystem, battlerId)]
- **low** *the AI's weather command knows retail's five weathers* -- ov10_0221D594: heavy rain, extremely harsh sunlight, snow and strong winds read as no weather to the AI scripts, though FIELD_CONDITION_RAIN_ALL and SUN_ALL hold the new bits. Low to medium, by the report. -- fixed in 453e2d07a: command 2E reads RAIN_ALL and SUN_ALL, heavy rain as rain and extremely harsh sunlight as sun, and snow as hail; strong winds still read no weather, no script asking what they change; ai_heavy_rain.json, the Hoothoot no longer using Rain Dance under Primordial Sea
- **low** *the AI's effectiveness counts the same-type bonus* -- ov10_0221D260 and ov10_0221D314 take ov12_02251D28 at a damage of 40, STAB and Adaptability included, and map back only 120, 240, 30 and 15: a neutral STAB move reads 60 (80 and up with Adaptability), so a script testing for 40 or 80 misreads it. Retail. -- closed with a reason: retail AI, kept for the AI design after the port. The fix: rate the effectiveness without the same-type bonus and Adaptability, so a neutral STAB move reads 40
- **low** *the AI's guess at a battler's last move uses the attacker's IVs* -- ov10_0221E498: a Hidden Power there gets the wrong type and power, and it measures against the AI's own target, so passing the target as the battler measures it against itself. Retail. -- closed with a reason: retail AI, kept for the AI design after the port. The fix: the battler's own IVs (GetBattlerVar with BMON_DATA_HP_IV + i), measured against the battler's own foe
- **info** *AI commands 62 and 63, and flag 30's Safari commands, never step* -- ov10_0221DDE8 and ov10_0221DDEC are empty and leave the script pointer where it is, so a script using them would hang; flag 30's Safari commands (0x3E, 0x3F) are the same. hg-engine's trainer_data.h names AI bits 11-13 F_ROAMING_MON, F_SAFARI_ZONE and F_CATCHING_DEMO, but the script sends flags 11-28 to an empty routine and has the roaming, Safari and catching routines at 29-31, of which only 29 is ever set. Retail. -- closed with a reason: retail AI, kept for the AI design after the port. The fix: step past the opcode (ov10_0221EF24(ctx, 1)) or drop flag 30's routine, and write in trainer_data that hg-engine's names for flags 11-13 do not match routines 29-31

## Found by the harness's core work, 2026-09-25

What the harness's move to melonDS DS (branch core-melondsds) found by
listening to its recordings, and what the fix for it on branch sound-heap
and that fix's review found on the way; then branch double-slot, which fixed
the double battle's row, and what its two reviews found. Engine layer unless
a row says otherwise.

- **high** *the intro's, the title's, the towns', the routes' and ordinary trainer battles' music never played* -- on every emulator: melonDS 0.9.3 and melonDS DS 1.3.1 in the harness, melonDS 1.1 on Paolo's desktop; the gyms' and the other battles' music, the clicks and the cries played. InitSoundData loads the sound archive's INFO and FAT blocks into the sound heap (SND_HEAP_SIZE, 0xBEAE0, static) for good, and every scene's music, groups and fanfares are loaded into what is left. The added species' banks, wave archives and file table entries (4c8176ea1, c9cf8f587) grew those blocks from HeartGold's 0x13954 bytes (0x139a0 of the heap) to 0x1bbec: 0x3520 was left after setup, 0x62e0 at the title and 0x61c0 on Route 29, where the intro's music needs 0xc328, the title's 0xb5a0, Route 29's 0xa95c and Elm's lab's 0xa0c4; the Violet Gym's 0x727c and Falkner's 0x7238 fit. (0x14b9c, the figure this row quoted in the tenth round's list, is an hg-engine build's, ~/Scaricati/test.nds: POKEMON HG, IPKE, 778 bank records and 1383 wave archives, its INFO 0xa210 and FAT 0xa98c.) Moved here from the tenth round's list, where it was found. -- closed in 51f7a1ca3 (NNSi_SndArcLoadBank in matching C, lib/NitroSystem/src/sndarc_loader.c), 86a7ace16 (the importer starts from HeartGold's own archive: 4c8176ea1's already held 65 of the cries it appended again), ae812433b (a number with a wave archive and no bank plays it on bank 1's instrument, hg-engine's pseudobank, and the added cries lose their banks), c0b0909bb (sdat.drop_unused_files) and 904c0baa3 (HeartGold's own cry banks 2 to 494 go the same way): the blocks cost 0x135a0 of the heap, 0x400 under HeartGold's. On the diagnostics build, melonDS DS: 0x75e0 left at the intro, 0x4480 at the title, 0x7560 on Route 29, in New Bark, Cherrygrove, Elm's lab and the Pokemon Center, 0xa4e0 in the Violet Gym, 0xae20 in a wild battle, 0x8400 against Bird Keeper Abe, each playing its music, SoulSilver the same; every cry started, retail's, the added ones (Baxcalibur, 1284, leaves 0x40 of its player heap, as Jynx does in HeartGold) and the forms'. No sound load HeartGold completes fails: a replay of the 17,881 it makes finds none, the tightest the Trainer Card over Ecruteak's music with 0x460 spare (HeartGold 0x60). The loader costs 0x40 of the main arena. Guarded by test_cries (the blocks' cost), test_boot (the intro's and the title's music, the title's cry), cry_added_species.json (an added cry starts) and continue_route29.json (Route 29's music, 5acad72ce).
- **medium** *a double battle asserts once one side is down to a single Pokemon* -- RawSpeedOrder (bb7c5c278) reads every battler's Speed through BattleSystem_GetPartyMon, the empty slot's too, and a slot with no one left to send has party index 6: Party_GetMonByIndex asserts. Twins Amy & Mimi, down to Shelmet with Karrablast's slot empty, frame 7763 of double_replacement.json played to the end on the diagnostics build, on this branch and on newgold's 5970c2470 alike; the harness then shows the communication error screen. In a build without the diagnostics GF_AssertFail returns unless a link is up, and the read goes past the party's six. Found fixing gym.py's double battles. -- closed in 757cb7ff3: RawSpeedOrder gives a place with no Pokemon, by retail's own test for one (its switch-in flag), Speed 0 instead of reading its party slot, keeping it in the order for a Future Sight whose fallen user stands in there (the reference's SortRawSpeedNonRNGArray makes the same read). A sweep of the reads through selectedMonIndex closed the others an empty place reaches: 20c6dbbb9 (the Bisharp defeat count read party slot 6 for a Future Sight landing from an empty place), 964c80801 (the form reversion of a switch into an empty place), 2f5a86315 (Mimicry when the terrain ends), aea865782 (a paradox boost's end), 108d5156a (a fallen ally's Flower Veil) and f0289d0de (Supersweet Syrup's and Teraform Zero's records, a read past the row with no assert); each fails its host test in test_empty_place or beside it when reverted. double_replacement.json plays the twins to the end since b3ed7e5d5, no assert; with 757cb7ff3's change reverted it asserts at frame 7774 and never wins. ## Found in the eleventh round, 2026-09-27

What the eleventh round's eight branches, their merge, the merge's review and
the replays found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look;
"decision" on Paolo. Engine layer unless a row says otherwise.

- **medium** *an interrupted first save after the conversion erased the save* -- the first save after an older save is converted writes the main slot and its footer first and the PC slot's footer some 800 frames later; cut between them, Save_GetSaveFilesStatus stopped at the lone main slot of now and said "The save file will be erased due to corruption or damage". The Berries pocket (7e3eb8e35) sends every save of Paolo's through the conversion once more. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in db4b1cefc: the older layouts are tried while no main slot or no PC slot reads as the layout in hand, and an older save loaded past a slot of now loads as the previous save file; test_save_legacy runs the real function, and the reviewer's cut-short copy boots to Continue
- **medium** *ingame_save.py could write out half a save* -- it waited for the core's .sav to stop changing, and the file does not change for seconds between the main slot's footer and the PC slot's when the boxes written are the bytes already there: the reviewer's second save of a layout-1 backup was copied with a main footer of now and the PC footer of before, and reloaded as a slot fail. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 0ee4766aa: it waits for SaveData.lastGoodSector to turn to the half written and reads the flash from the core's memory, the file's stillness kept for melonDS 0.9.3; the backup saved twice has both halves of now, counters 4 and 5, and reloads with statusFlags 0
- **low** *the save page and heaplens put an older save's bag in the layout of now* -- r11-items' layout left heaplens' saves.make writing items at the offsets of now into older saves, and the save page showing 100 Berry slots for an older save, which has 64, and naming a layout by its number. Found at the merge and by its review. -- fixed in 018fdd47c and 69db3afc4 (savedit.info() gives the layout's constant, read from include/save.h); 006241493 fills all 100 slots of a save of now and reads them back
- **info** *the Berries pocket is sized exactly* -- 100 slots for the 100 Berries the item data files there: a Berry added later needs another save layout, and test_reusable_tms, which counts them against the pocket, fails first. -- closed, no change: a limit written down, not a defect
- **info** *BattleContext has one byte of padding left* -- heldItemsCount fills the byte at 0x3255; heldItemsGiven at 0x325A moved the move conditions to 0x325B and the added moves' data to 0x326C, into the padding before the script buffer (0x4EEC), sizeof 0x5914 as before; one byte more still fits and the next grows the structure by four (mwcc, plain and NEWGOLD_DIAG). Found by the merge's review. -- closed in 834397985: the size check's note says so

At r12-integrate's tip the boot leaves 0x1BE8 of the arena on the plain builds and 0x6A8 on the diagnostics ones, which the next round's shares are sized from; more room would give a diagnostic up, Paolo's call (eight lines of the text ring instead of sixteen, 0x600; sixteen words of the assertion's stack instead of sixty-four, 0xC0). DIAGNOSTICS.md has the account (730de43df)

## Found in the twelfth round, 2026-10-02

What the twelfth round's nine branches, their merge, the merge's review and
the replays found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look;
"decision" on Paolo. Engine layer unless a row says otherwise.

- **medium** *a switch later in the same action as Baton Pass or Shed Tail passed again* -- BATTLE_STATUS_BATON_PASS stayed up until the action ended, so an Eject Pack, Emergency Exit or Wimp Out answering the entry, on either side, switched with it: the stat stages and status2 passed again, and the decoy under Shed Tail; a player's Baton Pass into an Intimidate set off a trainer's Eject Pack and the foe's replacement took its Leech Seed. Older than the round (df7e47aa4, b9cd07473). Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 96634d2c6: the switch command puts the flag down once InitSwitchWork has read it; baton_pass_then_eject_pack_passes_nothing.json, and shed_tail_then_eject_pack_passes_nothing.json (9e2956609) for the player's Shed Tail
- **medium** *flag 0 never marked down a status move an absorbing ability takes* -- a status move went from 002A past every absorber check, so Sand Attack into Earth Eater, Soak into Water Absorb or Irrigation and Thunder Wave into Lightning Rod scored as into anyone, and the round's absorbing fix made two more of them swallowed. Found by r12-ai (flag 0's Thunder Wave line, 0493-049D, knows Motor Drive and Volt Absorb but not Lightning Rod; the checks at 0035 and 296F are reached only by moves whose damage is weighed) and by the merge's review. -- fixed in 3910728a0 (engine) and 45f67b02e (New Gold: Irrigation; ca0182d8c's 'only damaging moves matter' corrected there): words from 2A26 send the status moves of an absorbed type aimed at the target to their type's check; ai_earth_eater_sand_attack.json. The Thunder Wave line at 0498 is no longer reached by a move these take, so Lightning Rod needs no place beside it
- **low** *Baton Pass dropped No Retreat's hold* -- 4ac87a213 ends every hold of the leaver's, No Retreat's on its own user with them, which Showdown's gen-9 noretreat passes. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 514fbad72; baton_pass_passes_no_retreat.json
- **low** *Mean Look and Jaw Lock ORed their trapper into a stale id* -- OPCODE_FLAG_ON into battlerIdMeanLook, as retail's Mean Look: a foe held by battler 2 and then by 0 named 2, so 0's switch freed nothing, and Baton Pass reads the id since 4ac87a213. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 4b0a2b3bd: every script that writes the id sets it
- **low** *Parental Bond's first-strike drag mark counted one Rocky Helmet* -- Battler_WillBeDraggedOut found the drag unless the user would fall to one helmet share, but with the second strike to come the helmet answers twice: a user between a sixth and two sixths of its HP had the first strike's Color Change muted, fell to the second helmet and dragged nothing. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in cd8b6c74d: two shares while the second strike is coming, a Jaboca Berry still one
- **low** *six gaps in the playthrough's harness* -- scene.py's field step did not pass flee; a leg run alone against another ROM played the legs before it on the default ROM; gym.py touched the party screen by save order, so a second pivot in one battle stalled at "already in battle!"; leg 02 could not check the running shoes, nor leg 03 the options it pressed blind; fight:N counts the move slots from 1 where teach: counts from 0, which the step list did not say. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in db2b367df, 6f7d4c725, 9e2956609 (gDiagPartyOrder, diagnostics builds only), 33702e617, 7aae89ed2 and 8fcb11d54, each with a test
- **info** *the round's texts said more than was seen* -- the fight stories of legs 03, 04 and 04b, and 4c's (Li takes the Cyndaquil alone on the fixed ROM); terrain_background.json's about, which reads a marker of the redraw, not the picture; DIAGNOSTICS.md's byte account, xmap.py's object sum against where the module ends; a docstring line 105 columns long. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 9a6599f41, e062ca26a, 730de43df and 4e2ee7e27
- **info** *test_trainer_ai_script did not check a line's argument count or where a jump lands* -- Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 5807910cd: each line against its command's handler, jumps on commands and tables on lines AI_TABLE_END ends; retail's Safari word at 2960 named
- **info** *a ROM target never patches the ARM9's assembler* -- only make's all target runs patch_mwasmarm for MWCCVER 2.0/sp2p2; the ARM7 and libsyscall patch sp2p3, and a ROM target, make tools and build_at.sh never patch sp2p2. Nothing shows here, the shared tools/mwccarm having both copies patched (one file); a fresh mwccarm would assemble the ARM9's .s files and every script with the line-ending and 0x400-incbin bugs the patcher fixes. Found by r12-build. -- fixed in 5f81276b6: every rule that runs the assembler waits, order-only, for a stamp of its own assembler patched (mwasmarm/<MWCCVER>.patched under the build directory), the ARM9's, the ARM7's, libsyscall's and nitrocrypto's 1.2 alike; d89ef3ead gives the patcher the sha1 its own patch of the 1.2 assembler makes, which it had wrong. test_build_rules checks every such rule in the make databases. The first build after the landing patches the shared 1.2 assembler and is best run alone
- **info** *build_at.sh says the ROM targets do not depend on the host tools* -- its line 43; since 02bc104af and this round every tool is a prerequisite of what it makes, the patcher included, and building the tools first serves older commits only. Found by r12-build. -- fixed in b92fc7a1f: it says the tools are prerequisites of what they make and every assembler is patched, so its tools step serves older commits only, and that step patches the assembler as make's all target does

Found by r12-abilities. -- fixed in 454b68cf0: Forecast only picks the weather's form, and without it Castform goes back to its Normal Form and type (Previsioni, and the reference's branch); castform_loses_forecast.json, Gastro Acid in the sun

Reachable: trainer Erin has a Cherrim. Found by r12-abilities. -- fixed in d59d0c871: the Sunshine Form only with Flower Gift in the sun, no Cloud Nine or Air Lock out, the Overcast Form otherwise (Regalfiore; the reference's BattleFormChangeCheck.c:96 asks no ability, which was not copied); cherrim_loses_flower_gift.json

- **info** *ingame_save.py's melonDS 0.9.3 path cannot work* -- Arch's libretro-melonds 0.9.3 exposes no save memory and never writes its .sav (the md5 unchanged 5,000 frames after a finished save, after unload and after deinit), so the path always ends "the flash did not change"; its docstring says it works there. scene.py's save step says it needs melonDS DS. Found by r12-play-opening. -- fixed in 861304c0d: it refuses melonDS 0.9.3 before playing, an in-game save needing melonDS DS, and its docstrings and DIAGNOSTICS.md say 0.9.3 never writes the save back
- **low** *savedit.py blames the save when the plain build is stale* -- it sizes the save blocks from build/heartgold.us, and with only the diagnostics build current it said "neither half of the flash holds a valid save": falkner, rolls_forced_high and both walk_* scenarios failed so until the plain build was redone. build_behind() exists and does not feed this error. Found by r12-play-opening. -- fixed in e2a83cd67: savedit names a build behind the tree, not the save

- **info** *a terrain's start plays no animation of its own* -- the latest games play one (grass, sparks, mist, psychic waves) before the field stays covered; the reference has the background swap alone, so there is nothing to bring over. A start effect could be built from the game's own particles (Thunder Wave's sparks, Ingrain's growth, Mist, Psychic's background) as a status sub-animation, which needs this game's status-animation table grown past entry 40 and overlay 7's table made matching C first. Decision. Found by r12-visuals. -- fixed in 50d5ad0fa and 22a9c1a48, as Paolo decided on 2026-10-02: four battle animations made from retail's particles, members 50 to 53 of a/0/6/1 (hg-engine's BATTLE_ANIMATION_*_TERRAIN numbers; the shipped fifty kept byte for byte): Grassy with Grass Whistle's tint, Ingrain's motes and Grass Knot's leaves, Misty with Mist in a pink tint, Electric with Spark's tint and Discharge's lightning, Psychic with Psychic's waves, played by subscript 347 before the background and the line. 3b6951d5a stops the borrowed move animation hg-engine's subscript played after the line, and fa6c8b6f4 hides the health boxes as a weather's start does, after 5545f7b09 made what an animation hides matching C. Seen in core.shot()'s frames; terrain_animation.json reads the animation, the move-animation count and the hidden boxes
- **info** *ChangePermanentBackground with mixed operands follows them literally* -- a terrain's background with the battle's own ground would draw platforms, where hg-engine hides them under every added background. No script does it. Found by r12-visuals. -- closed with a reason in c9b5621b1: no script mixes them. All 13 calls pair a terrain's background with that terrain's ground (CreateTerrainOverlay, five) or the battle's own with its own (HandleTerrainEnd and Defog, four each); following hg-engine's LoadDifferentBattleBackground, which puts no platforms under any added background, would cost overlay-12 bytes for a case no script reaches, and the commit's test fails the day a script mixes them

- **info** *flag 9 gives every move but the weathers the first-turn +5 unless the sun is up* -- its dispatch falls into Sunny Day's lines, and the block at 2A0B keeps it. Retail's. Found by r12-ai. -- closed with a reason: retail AI, kept for the AI design after the port
- **low** *a foe taught at the first command prompt has already chosen its turn-one slot* -- it chooses as the prompt comes up, before fight:1:0 stops and teach: writes, and keeps the slot, not the move: a scenario that empties a foe's other slots has its first turn decided by the slot picked, which moved with r12-lab's 0x40-shorter static module and broke roost_pure_flying_is_normal ("The wild Chansey's - is disabled!"). Found by r12-lab. -- fixed in a63dff1f7: teach: lets an answer on its way land, writes the move and has a battler that chose from this turn's request asked again (SSI_STATE_13 or 14 back to 3) when it has a usable move, a locked or encored move left alone; teach_after_the_foes_choice.json, the wild Chansey that gave slot 2 using the Lick it was taught, the slot checked since 7a2d4b2aa (the merge's review). 923020d40's teaching into every slot is no longer needed and stays

- **info** *diag.h says gDiagAssertStack holds sixteen words* -- DIAG_ASSERT_STACK_WORDS is 64 and markers.py reads 64. Found by r12-lab. -- fixed in 976b17ef6

Found by r12-items. -- fixed in 1ebf68d73: the eighteen Gems burn too, found by their hold effect (Bruciatutto from the sixth generation; Showdown's gen-9 incinerate; the reference's IS_ITEM_GEM); incinerate_burns_a_gem.json
- **low** *Crafty Shield lets through status moves with no protect flag* -- Truccodifesa has it stop Sketch and Play Nice, which Protect lets through, and Showdown's gen-9 craftyshield every status move aimed at a Pokemon (Roar, Whirlwind, Transform, Mean Look, Block, Psych Up, Role Play and others); GuardStopsMove asks the protect flag first, Bestow alone excepted since 7967ac0f3. Not traced further (an ally's status moves). Found by r12-items. -- fixed in 2a69b4edd: it stops every status move aimed at a Pokemon (Showdown's gen-9 craftyshield, Truccodifesa); crafty_shield_stops_mean_look.json, Perish Song still going through
- **info** *Pokemon Central's Cediregalo and Sostituto (bambola) disagree on Bestow and a substitute* -- the effect paragraph keeps the fifth generation's failure, the table the sixth's bypass. Found by r12-items. -- closed with a reason: the later rule is kept, as Bulbapedia, Showdown's bypasssub and the reference's bestow/ignore_substitute.c have it (7967ac0f3 says so)
- **low** *a trapped Pokemon's refused run reads like a failed escape* -- CantEscape's msg_0197_00794, for Wrap, Mean Look, Ingrain, Fairy Lock and Sky Drop, says "You couldn't get away!" as 00042 does for a failed try, which spends the turn; retail's line was "Can't escape!", and 248d7a9c7 took the engine's bank 197 text. The latest games' line is to confirm on Pokemon Central. Found by r12-play-violet. -- fixed in f822baa6e: "You can't escape!" (msg_0197 row 01948, Scarlet and Violet's English common text, where it sits beside "You couldn't get away!"; Pokemon Central quotes neither), and the command prompt comes back; trapped_run_cant_escape.json, which checks since 7a28f8e19 that no turn was spent, and gym.py's may_run knows the line since a7566df43 (both the merge's review)
- **info** *scene.py's step mode does not quiet the core* -- 'scene.py SAVE OUT STEPS' does not call gym.quiet() as --scenario does, so melonDS DS's retro_get_memory_data chatter fills stdout, 15 MB and 600,000 lines a leg. Found by r12-play-violet. -- fixed in b1980c7ee: the step mode keeps the core quiet, stderr too, where the chatter is (not stdout, as the row said; 5,052 core lines for one field step before)
- **medium** *the playthrough's bot cannot catch* -- so legs 4c and 5 start from savedit-edited parties (Cyndaquil, Spearow, Mareep, Geodude, Gastly and Zubat at 10; Geodude, Mareep, Onix, Shinx and a savedit Cyndaquil at 13): a lone Cyndaquil at the cap lost to Li's Meditite and to Falkner's Hoothoot. A catch step in gym.py (BAG, the Poke Balls pocket, the ball, USE, B at the nickname) and gotos that train the catches would replace the edits. Found by r12-play-violet. -- fixed in 00941cf00, e0b14209c, cf8c00e0f, ab8c94a2d and 37404742f: gym.fight(catch=) weakens a wanted wild Pokemon with its weakest damaging move and throws the bag's first ball, scene.py's catch: names the species (catch_route29.json), and the chain catches and trains its own Hoothoot, Geodude and Misdreavus beside the Cyndaquil, Elder Li won with no edit; leg 05's savedit --level, 07b's --train and 09b's --teach are rows of the thirteenth round
- **info** *gym.py's closing party list can read a Pokemon half decrypted* -- in the fix's Whitney replay (.rounds/round12/land/fix/gyms/whitney.out) slot 3 read as species 19423 at exp 2775330619 holding item 63671, a frame having ended inside the game's decryption; scene.py's party expectations wait for party.sealed since 1c17ffa8d, and gym.py's list, which whitney.py prints too, reads once. Devkit. Found by the docs step. -- fixed in 9ec4c595e: the list waits for every Pokemon sealed, as scene.py's party expectations do, and scene.py reads its party through the same loop since 52512f756

## Found in the thirteenth round, 2026-10-03

What the thirteenth round's seven branches, their merge, the merge's review
and the replays found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look;
"decision" on Paolo. Engine layer unless a row says otherwise.

- **medium** *Sleep Talk kept the fourth generation's refusals* -- it took the fifth's Gravity and Heal Block rule (c0d647f5c) and still asked StruggleCheck for Disable, Torment, Taunt, Imprison, Throat Chop, the Choice items, Gorilla Tactics, Belch and the Assault Vest; from the fifth generation it calls a disabled move and the moves Torment and Imprison block, under Taunt the called status move works, and from the seventh a sound move it calls under Throat Chop fails (Sonnolalia; Showdown's gen-9 sleeptalk refuses only its nosleeptalk and charge moves). Found by r13-moves and the merge's review. -- fixed in 6dcd0bab6 (a sound move another calls fails under Throat Chop, Metronome's, Copycat's and Assist's too) and 2ce72774d (Sleep Talk calls what its user could not choose); sleep_talk_under_a_choice_item.json and sleep_talk_calls_into_throat_chop.json, each failing on the parent's ROM
- **medium** *the form scenarios could not fail on the wrong form* -- they read the battler's species, which Castform's and Cherrim's forms leave alone, so castform_snow passed with snow giving the wrong form. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in f1f13f174: the diagnostics' battler view shows the form, in its padding (28 bytes still, the plain build unchanged), and the three scenarios expect it
- **low** *a caught Pokemon's item could be bagged twice* -- after Tricks and a Knock Off, catching the wild Pokemon left its slot's itemsLost mark, so the item it kept went to the bag as well. Found by the merge's review, with a probe. -- fixed in c4adcece7: CaughtMonKeepsItem clears the slot's marks as it rewrites its entry; the probe's case is in test_battle_context's fixture
- **low** *an absorbed move spent the Micle Berry's boost* -- the accuracy roll used it up before the absorbing ability took the move. - **low** *teach_after_the_foes_choice took the foe's slot on trust* -- Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 7a2d4b2aa: the battler view shows the slot each battler chose, and the scenario checks it is 2 before teaching
- **low** *trapped_run_cant_escape did not check that the turn was kept* -- Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 7a28f8e19: the Chansey's Mean Look is printed once in the battle; played with FIGHT for RUN, it fails

Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 9b4909e2f: the team the chain brings, its moves chosen by gym.py's rule (4ce088156), beats Proton unedited, at the third attempt where the leg was written and on the merged tree (67,612 frames as the joined chain plays it). In play, Route 31's Pokemon at 4 to 7 pay them 15 to 35 points a battle, some 30 battles each, a training leg or two apiece after Elder Li. Found by r13-play-catch. -- fixed in 9af39f13f: legs 04j and 04k train the Misdreavus, the Cyndaquil and a caught Mareep on Route 32's first grass, and leg 05 beats Falkner unedited, at the fifth attempt where it was written and at the first on the merged tree (30,009 frames)

- **info** *the bot never learns a fifth move* -- it gives the new one up every time: its Hoothoot never got Confusion, the Geodude Bulldoze, the Cyndaquil Quick Attack. Devkit. Found by r13-play-catch. -- fixed in 4ce088156: forgets() keeps the strongest damaging move of each type, then the other damaging moves, then the status moves, the older one on a tie, and fight() works the battle's party menu; learn_a_fifth_move.json, a Cyndaquil forgetting Smokescreen for Quick Attack and a Hoothoot giving Reflect up, and in the playthrough the Hoothoot's Confusion, the Geodude's Bulldoze, Rock Throw and Smack Down and the Misdreavus's Psybeam
