Hoothoot was caught!"
- **info** *scene.py's goto and field can hand back control just before a map script runs* -- goto waits 16 frames on its goal, so a coord event's script has begun and its message is not through (leg 09: VAR_FARFETCHD1_STICKS2 set, FLAG_FARFETCHD_NOTICED_YOU not yet); field returns on the first frame the player can move, before an OnFrameTable script takes over (leg 07b, Kurt's). The legs wait (field after the goto, wait:30 then field); a fix in goto or field would move every scenario's timing. Devkit. Found by r13-play-azalea. -- fixed in 6f40a4651: field goes on while a frame-table entry of the map is due, and goto, once on its goal, plays through what the goal set off until the player can move; field_waits_for_kurts_frame_script.json and goto_waits_for_what_its_goal_sets_off.json fail with the scene.py before it. The legs' own waits are redundant now, and kept
- **info** *markers.PROMPTS takes turn one's wait for the AI for the command prompt* -- a gDiagBattlePrompt of 2 is SSI_STATE_2 on turn one, the player waiting for the AI's choice before the menu is up, so fight:N:0 stops before the prompt and the first FIGHT touch can land on nothing; it works because the touch is retried. Devkit. Found by r13-lab. -- fixed in 1eb4b7d81: PROMPTS[2] reads as waiting for the foe's choice, and gym.fight, the partner's FIGHT, the throw, the relief and trace.py act at prompt 1 only. fight:N:T still stops at 1 or 2, on purpose: stopping at 1 alone moved smack_down_terrain_fatal_answer's rolls, its teach: asking the foe again, and the commit and gym.fight's comment say so
- **info** *the devkit README says melonDS 0.9.3 writes no save back while it runs* -- it never writes it back at all; ingame_save.py's docstrings and DIAGNOSTICS.md say so since 861304c0d. Found by r13-lab. -- fixed in c526987c1: it says melonDS 0.9.3 never writes the save back, not even at unload, and that the save step refuses that core
- **info** *test_scenarios plays three scenarios at once, with no switch* -- WORKERS is 3, above a round agent's two emulators; three of the round's agents wrapped it to play two, two played theirs apart and r13-build left them out. Devkit. Found by r13-items. -- fixed in 7b6a46c34: NEWGOLD_WORKERS sets how many scenarios play at once, 3 when unset; the README and test_scenarios' docstring say so
- **info** *the wait-on-PIDs recipe does not split under zsh* -- 'for p in $PIDS; do while kill -0 $p ...' got one word in the agents' shell, so kill -0 failed at once and four scene.py runs played together for half a minute; it needs ${=PIDS} or an array. Found by r13-abilities. -- closed with a reason: the recipe is in the rounds' prompts and the memory notes, not in the tree; the next round's prompt is where it changes
- **info** *Paolo's gyms README says chuck.sav has not been played headless* -- chuck.json plays it to the badge since 0a0a5caa6. Found by r13-lab, and named in the falkner.sav row before. -- open, Paolo's file
- **info** *the patcher took its own patched 1.2 assembler for an unknown one* -- its table gave sha1 3395ac5d for the patched 1.2/base, where its two bytes make 049af742, so the second build refused it as "Unsupported mwasmarm.exe version" and SoulSilver stopped; upstream never patched 1.2. Found by r13-build. -- fixed in d89ef3ead; test_build_rules patches a copy of each unpatched assembler and runs the patcher on it again
- **info** *a lone ASM object made as the goal builds what files_for_compile needs with its own variables* -- make hands a target's variables down to its prerequisites, so `make build/heartgold.us/asm/nitrocrypto.o` would assemble the out-of-date battle and field scripts, about a thousand, with MWCCVER 1.2/sp2p3 (a dry run); a ROM build reaches them through a C object first and is not touched. `private` on Makefile:17's MWCCVER may fix it, if the target's own second expansion still sees the value. To confirm. Found by r13-build. -- fixed in 92f47143d: MWCCVER is private on Makefile:17, so a lone nitrocrypto.o no longer hands its assembler to its prerequisites (a dry run listed 3,694 script commands under 1.2/sp2p3 before, under 2.0/sp2p2 after); test_build_rules dry-runs it
- **info** *sub's and libsyscall's makefiles still patch the assembler themselves* -- their phony patch_mwasmarm, and make all's own step, only read since the top level patches first: redundant and harmless. A simplification. Found by r13-build. -- fixed in d5271cf50: only the patch stamps patch the assembler; make's all, sub's and libsyscall's steps are gone, and the phony patch_mwasmarm stays in common.mk for build_at.sh's older commits; test_build_rules reads the three recipes
- **low** *a Pokemon transformed into Castform or Cherrim changes form with the weather* -- BtlCmd_Transform copies species, ability and form, and Battler_CheckWeatherFormChange asks no STATUS2_TRANSFORM; Previsioni from the fifth generation has a transformed Pokemon keep its form whatever the weather, and Showdown's gen-9 Forecast and Flower Gift return early for one; Cherrim to confirm on Regalfiore. A few bytes. Found by r13-abilities. -- fixed in bf5648e9e: Battler_CheckWeatherFormChange passes over a transformed Castform or Cherrim (Previsioni from the fifth generation; Showdown's gen-9 Forecast and Flower Gift); castform_transformed_keeps_form.json and cherrim_transformed_keeps_form.json. The row's premise did not hold on this tree: Transform had copied no ability since 3742bcfb2, which 258eaeed8 puts back, and a7c79c100 extends the guard to Arceus and Silvally, both rows of the fourteenth round
- **info** *Soundproof, Bulletproof, Telepathy, Evaporate and the ability refusals speak through a guard and over a target out of reach* -- ov12_0224BC2C lets BLOCKED_BY_SOUNDPROOF and BLOCKED_BY_ABILITY past every DID_NOT_HIT bit: a sound move into a Soundproof holder that is protecting prints the ability's line, not the guard's, and one aimed at a holder underground the ability, not a miss; the status refusals among them (Comatose, Shields Down, the Pastel, Sweet, Flower and Aroma Veils) answer a move that missed the accuracy roll too. In the ninth generation semi-invulnerability comes first, Protect's TryHit before the abilities', and the status refusals act only when the status is set, after accuracy. Evaporate is konefr's, New Gold's layer. Found by r13-abilities. -- fixed in f48dc4533: RefusalSilencedBy puts each refusal at the ninth generation's step (Showdown's trySpreadMoveHit): Armor Tail, Queenly Majesty and Dazzling before the move reaches anyone, Soundproof's script (Soundproof, Bulletproof, Telepathy, Evaporate), Oblivious against Taunt and the absorbing abilities at TryHit, silenced by a guard or a target out of reach, and Comatose, Shields Down and the four Veils only when the move hits; test_refusal_order runs ov12_0224BC2C on the host, and soundproof_after_a_guard_and_out_of_reach.json and status_refusal_after_the_roll.json check the miss since 004edda6a (the merge's review). Evaporate, konefr's, follows Soundproof's place with his code untouched, which KONEFR-NOTES asks him about
- **info** *Psychic Terrain's and Safety Goggles' refusals come after the accuracy roll* -- in the ninth generation they are TryHit and TryImmunity, both before accuracy; here a powder move that missed a goggles holder reads as a miss. Found by r13-abilities. -- fixed in bb299b68c: Safety Goggles refuse at the TryHit step and Psychic Terrain before the guard (Showdown's onTryHitPriority 4 to Protect's 3), only a target out of reach silencing it; psychic_terrain_and_goggles_before_the_roll.json
- **info** *Incinerate's description names Berries only* -- msg_0749 row 534, hg-engine's d0380a487 text ("rendering a held Berry useless"), where Gems burn too since 1ebf68d73; Bruciatutto's latest text has Berries or some kinds of items. test_moves ties the move texts to hg-engine's, so a change needs an override in import_moves. Found by r13-items. -- fixed in d2e826094: "The user attacks with fire. A certain held item, such as a Berry, is burned up." (Bruciatutto, and Scarlet and Violet's English text), through import_moves' DESCRIPTIONS_FIXED; test_moves fails without it
- **info** *itemsLost keeps a battler's last loss only* -- a wild Pokemon that loses two items, two hand-overs each knocked off or a handed item knocked off after it took another by Magician or Covet, has the second alone bagged; a ponytail note in GiveBackHeldItems says so. A bit per party slot, set where the item is lost, would fix it. Found by r13-items. -- fixed in 6f9dd6a12: the per-battler itemsLost is gone, a loss now marked by the Taken bit of the owner's slot, one mark per item, Knock Off and Corrosive Gas marking it whoever holds the item; knock_off_a_handed_item_and_then_another.json bags the Silk Scarf after two Tricks and two Knock Offs
- **info** *a Berry handed to a wild Pokemon and burnt by Incinerate is not bagged* -- NoteHeldItemUsedUp takes it as eaten; Raggiro's exception speaks of the Berries the wild Pokemon eats itself, and Pokemon Central does not cover burning. To confirm. Found by r13-items. -- closed with a reason: kept, a burnt Berry gone everywhere. Raggiro's ninth generation sends handed items to the bag and excepts the Berries the wild Pokemon consumes, Bruciatutto has the Berry destroyed and not to be had back by Recycle or Harvest, and the player's own Berry burnt by Incinerate is not given back either (incinerate_burns_the_party_s_berry.json); 9a9779850 pins it in play, "Oran Berry was burned up!" and no Berry in the bag. If Paolo reads Raggiro literally, only a Berry eaten being excepted, the change is one branch in NoteHeldItemUsedUp

Found by r13-moves. -- fixed in 81ce4d8f9: Berserk joins Color Change and Anger Shell's check (CheckColorChangeAngerShellAndBerserk), armed by Battler_ArmRetreat, and answers once after the last strike (Furore; Showdown's totalDamage) with "Berserk raised its Sp. Atk!"; a Pokemon being dragged out still answers, neither source excepting it; berserk_after_the_last_strike.json
- **low** *Take Heart aims at the user's ally and has the protect flag* -- the reference's data/Moves.c at d0380a487 (RANGE_ALLY, FLAG_PROTECT), where the script raises the user's stats and Showdown's target is the user, with no protect flag: in a double battle the partner is the target, so its Protect stops the move, and since 2a69b4edd a Crafty Shield on that side does too. The fix is in the move importer. Found by r13-moves. -- fixed in 862bbf415: the importer gives the record RANGE_USER and the Snatch flag, without the protect, Magic Coat and Mirror Move flags (Baldimpulso; Showdown's gen-9 takeheart); take_heart_aimed_at_its_user.json
- **info** *Nature Power calls the fourth generation's table* -- sNaturePowerMoveTable (asm/overlay_12_0226C324.s): Earthquake on plain ground, Seed Bomb on grass; a forced wild Chansey's Nature Power on Route 29 became Earthquake. Naturforza's later generations (Tri Attack, Energy Ball, the terrains' moves) to check. To confirm. Found by r13-moves. -- fixed in c3e8705e8: the eighth generation's table (Naturforza; the ninth has the move unusable): a terrain's move first, Energy Ball, Moonblast, Thunderbolt or Psychic, then Tri Attack on plain ground and indoors, Earth Power on sand and rock, Energy Ball in grass, Power Gem in caves, Ice Beam on snow and ice, Hydro Pump on water; nature_power_eighth_generation.json. Its target, and Secret Power's and Camouflage's tables, are rows of the fourteenth round

Found by r13-moves. -- fixed in c83450ada: no added move aimed at its user, its side or the field gets the protect flag (the importer's UNGUARDED_TARGETS, 28 records, each checked on Pokemon Central); Bide, retail's, keeps it. 3d3cdaa93 gives 13 records the Snatch flag Pokemon Central gives them, and b30969756 and 27d5a5632 take the Magic Coat and Mirror Move flags off the same kind of move, rows of the fourteenth round
- **info** *msg_0197 row 794 is printed by nothing* -- the engine's "You couldn't get away!" for a trapped run, which row 01948 replaced in f822baa6e. Found by r13-moves. -- closed with a reason: kept, as test_battle_messages pins bank 197's rows as the engine has them
- **low** *Instruct repeated Nature Power* -- Nature Power was not counted as a move that calls another, so Instruct had the Chansey use it again. Found by r13-moves. -- fixed in c70918cb5: "But it failed!"; instruct_fails_on_nature_power.json
- **low** *experience is shared out among the Pokemon that fought* -- BattleScript_GainerExp divides the award by expMonsCnt, as hg-engine does, the fifth generation's rule, where from the sixth each one that fought gets the whole share (Bulbapedia's formula); the playthrough's Geodude got half with the Cyndaquil swapped in. To confirm on Pokemon Central's experience page. Found by r13-play-catch. -- closed with a reason: Paolo 2026-10-04: kept as HGSS and hg-engine have it; a question for konefr (KONEFR-NOTES), who balanced New Gold on the divided share. The fourteenth round's change to the whole share was not landed
- **info** *two gaps the catching bot met* -- a BAG touch made the moment the prompt came up was ignored twice in three tries, and a party read caught a Pokemon half decrypted (species 17223), so swap: never saw its trade happen and swapped back and forth. Found by r13-play-catch. -- fixed in 108358635 and 52512f756
- **info** *gym.fight chose a move a foe's ability turned away again and again* -- in leg 07b a level-19 Geodude used Bulldoze ten times into Proton's Koffing, each answered by Levitate, the picker scoring by the type chart alone. Found by r13-play-azalea. -- fixed in f493bb585: wasted() reads the no-effect line, and the move is not chosen against that foe again unless nothing else is left
- **info** *an empty Spinarak station asserts* -- stepping onto a station with no cart reaches GF_ASSERT in BeginAzaleaGymSpinarakRide (retail's, compiled out of the plain build), which would fail a scenario's "asserts 0" on the diagnostics build; the legs never step on one. From reading the code, not seen in play. Found by r13-play-azalea. -- closed with a reason: the diagnostics build keeps retail's assert. No state a visit to Azalea Gym can reach puts the player on an empty station (60 states searched from the walkable tiles, azalea.c's routes and both switches), the gym is set again on every entry by warp and a Continue keeps it as saved; only an edited save gets there, as gyms/bugsy.sav does, where a step on (9,9) counts one assert and nothing else happens. The row was wrong in one point: PM_KEEP_ASSERTS keeps GF_ASSERT in the plain build, where it returns at once outside wireless play. The ride data it leaks there is a row of the fourteenth round

## Found in the fourteenth round, 2026-10-04

What the fourteenth round's nine branches, their merge, the merge's review
and its fixes found. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look;
"decision" on Paolo. Engine layer unless a row says otherwise.

- **high** *two Magic Bounce holders sent a move back and forth without end* -- the bounce (ov12_0224C204) is asked again of the Pokemon a bounced move goes to, and b30969756's guard covered only a move aimed at its user: a Pikachu and a wild Chansey, both with Magic Bounce, printed the bounce line in turn for 61,896 frames and the turn never ended, and a holder's move into a Magic Coat came back, was sent off again by the holder's ability and hit the coat's user. Older than the round. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 81b14a64a: a move sent back is marked (BATTLE_STATUS2_MAGIC_COAT, cleared as the move ends) and not sent back again, as in every game (Showdown's hasBounced); magic_bounce_sends_a_move_back_once.json
- **medium** *a Magic Bounce holder sent its own move back at itself forever* -- 33 added moves aimed at the user, its side, the field or its ally carried the engine's Magic Coat flag, and ov12_0224C204 asked only the target, the user itself: a Magic Bounce Pikachu's Victory Dance bounced for 61,826 frames and the battle stuck. Found by r14-moves. -- fixed in b30969756: the importer leaves the flag off those moves (Pokemon Central: not reflected), and the bounce asks that the target is not the attacker; magic_bounce_keeps_its_own_move.json, which checks the Defense and Speed rises too since 004edda6a
- **medium** *Transform copied no ability since 3742bcfb2* -- BtlCmd_Transform copies the first 40 bytes of the BattleMon, and 3742bcfb2 widened the ability to a u16 outside them, so a Pokemon that used Transform kept its own ability (Imposter's script copies it itself); the reference copies it after its loop (HandleTransform). Found by r14-abilities. -- fixed in 258eaeed8: transform_copies_ability.json, a wild Ditto copying a Levitate Pikachu and making its Mud-Slap miss
- **medium** *an empty hand's stale held-item tag marked a hand-over that never happened* -- NoteHeldItemUsedUp keeps a used-up item's tag for Recycle and Harvest, and NoteHeldItemGiven read it without asking whether the hand held anything: after a trainer battle a Pokemon that had eaten its own Oran Berry and then been given Leftovers by Bestow, or Tricked them, held the Berry again, and in a wild battle a Focus Sash used up and then a Trick lost the Sash, nothing going to the bag. A regression 65aa5e08b brought, 47f4fe6cf having had these right. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 7e6043877: an empty hand's tag marks nothing, and the hand that gets nothing gets no tag; three cases in test_battle_context's NOTE_FIXTURE. Its Sticky Barb half, the Barb leaving its tag behind and a Barb stolen back marking an eaten item as taken, is fixed in b42a8600e
- **medium** *the Dex forms scenario checked only the cry* -- dex_forms_galarian_slowpoke.json expected gDiagCrySpecies alone, the form's name, types, sprites and AREA page being shots deleted on a pass; the review saw them right in kept shots. Found by the merge's review. -- fixed in 40a66d872 and 1296b7908: four diagnostics-only markers for what the FORMS page draws, its types and what the AREA page reads (the plain ROM unchanged), and the scenario expects the Galarian Slowpoke as Psychic alone with no area, then the Slowpoke as Water and Psychic with its areas
- **medium** *Rotom's appliance forms are Electric/Ghost* -- personal.json's rows 503 to 507, Heat to Mow Rotom, keep HeartGold's Electric/Ghost, where hg-engine at d0380a487 (data/Species.c, SPECIES_503 Electric/Fire) and the games from the fifth generation give Electric with Fire, Water, Ice, Flying and Grass: a Heat Rotom from the Rotom Catalog fights as Electric/Ghost, and the Dex's FORMS page shows it so. The fix is in the importer. Found by r14-dex-forms. -- fixed in abb5914c1: the species importer takes the five appliance rows' types from the reference's SPECIES_503 to 507, the same at d0380a487 and a477c662f, and no other row changes, every other form's types checked against the reference and the latest games; rotom_heat_is_fire.json, a Heat Rotom given by savedit --form (2cfb15d4f), reads "It's super effective!" from a forced Psyduck's Water Gun, where the parent's ROM printed no effectiveness line

Found by r14-moves. -- fixed in 27d5a5632, the flag off those moves, Court Change, Fairy Lock, Magic Room, Wonder Room and Power Shift keeping it as Pokemon Central has them copied; the merge's review found those five and retail's Trick Room still copied from the user itself, never noted for anyone else, and since d0cf69e01 no move is noted for Mirror Move when its target is its user; mirror_move_does_not_copy_its_user_s_own_move.json and mirror_move_does_not_copy_its_user_s_own_magic_room.json

Def 230, while Rock Tomb knocked the Quilava out, and it never uses a status move or the bag's Potions, which is why the bot needs levels well above a player's for konefr's gyms; a damage estimate from the stats, and healing items, would cut the training. Devkit. Found by r14-play-whitney. -- fixed in 95f71b612: a turn weighed by the damage formula, the type chart, the abilities a player is told of, items and accuracy, the foe's moves the same way; the bag's Potions, status moves where they pay and switches (gym_brings_in_the_pokemon_that_wins.json, gym_gives_hp_back_where_it_wins.json, gym_uses_a_status_move_where_it_pays.json); after it 801810e41 (the foe's priority moves and the lowest roll), 7d0cf97a4 and d306a876c (the bench healed, the bag's HP kept for trainers), 15490093e, 453884897 and c5a897ed5
- **info** *the playthrough stops at Goldenrod's Radio Card* -- the legs to Whitney are drafts in .rounds/round14/tmp/battle/drafts (11 and 11b, Route 35's eight trainers; 12, the gym's four; 12b, Whitney and her Lass's walk): leg 11 ran 203,276 frames and its goto to Bird Keeper Bryan timed out, and the round-13 team at 23 to 27 lost to Whitney nine times running and to Lass Carrie six times; her five are 28 to 30, and the team needs the cap, 30, and likely better play. Devkit. Found by r14-play-whitney. -- fixed in 81437ce8f: legs 11 to 12b play Route 35, the National Park, Route 36 and the Goldenrod Gym to Whitney's Plain Badge; the bot's team beats her only with savedit --train 36 (e68a4557e), a row of its own (leg 12b)

## Found in the fifteenth round, 2026-10-04

What the fifteenth round's eight landed branches and its two proposals, the TM
work and the EV/IV trainer found, landed together in 5fec2af87 on 2026-10-05
with no review lenses (Paolo: one review over the three, in the sixteenth
round); the decisions Paolo took for them; and what he found playing that ROM
on 2026-10-05. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a second look;
"decision" on Paolo. Engine layer unless a row says otherwise.

- **critical** *a wild battle never starts with the emulator's JIT on* -- on Paolo's phone, 5fec2af87's plain ROM trimmed to the header's size and a save edited with --train 55, the music plays, the field stays dimmed by the encounter effect and the bottom screen black (.rounds/round16/paolo_wild_battle_hang.png); with the JIT off the battle starts (Paolo, 2026-10-05). The same picture as the melonDS DS JIT hang core.py warns of: a defect only a JIT shows, and perhaps the hardware, likely a cache not flushed or a race in the encounter effect or the battle's load, where retail runs with the JIT. Paolo wants it to work with the JIT, before anything else. Found by Paolo. -- fixed in 1782fb651 (matching) and 5bda07b01: melonDS's JIT inlines a Thumb PC-relative literal at an address 2 mod 4 rotated, after DSProt's self-rewriting put the aligned word on its written-literals list; 0ba297111 had moved DSProt's wrappers onto ov120's literal (retail has the same hazard off its common path). The game no longer runs DSProt (lib/dsprot kept, its flashcart pass still tested); wild_battle_jit.json (b5021791f, "jit": true) fails before and passes after, and Paolo's trimmed ROM and save start the battle in Route 30's grass with the JIT on
- **medium** *the EV/IV trainer's goodbye has no frame after her shop* -- after a Bottle Cap is bought from her in Cherrygrove's Pokemon Center, "Come back anytime!" is drawn in a window without its frame, and the top screen's right part, about x 184 to 255, stays black (.rounds/round16/paolo_evnpc_after_shop.png); Cherrygrove's own mart does not do it, so it is her script's (844b55090, SpecialMartBuy 30): the frame's tiles or the mart's top-screen window not put back as the mart closes. Found by Paolo on his phone. -- fixed in 7df4a0d3d: her script let her message box go before the special mart, as retail's clerks do; ev_iv_trainer scenarios (1f3bb4aad, f573f21fb's field-tile expectation) see the framed box after the shop, the app and CANCEL
- **medium** *the Battle Hall's search could hang the game* -- retail's ov80_02237448 turned back to the stretch's first set one set early, so a search started on the last set never came back to its start and, once the type's other sets had all been picked in the round, went on for ever: Dragon, three sets in the first stretch, chosen at ranks 1 and 2 of a double challenge hung on one start in 154. Retail's own, the Hall being Platinum's and HeartGold's alone. Found by r15-hall. -- fixed in 58c7cbf71: the search goes round the whole stretch, its last set picked as any other; test_battle_hall_sets plays a double challenge's three battles of one type from every start, which retail's search never ends
- **medium** *only 100 of the 340 machines could be had, and the TM case held 101* -- hg-engine's machines were HeartGold's TM01 to HM08, a second HM07, TM00, its TM093 to TM100, Scarlet and Violet's TM100 to TM229 and Sword and Shield's TR00 to TR99, in its order; nothing gave the 240 past HM08, and NUM_BAG_TMS_HMS stayed 101. Decision. Found by the TM proposal (.rounds/round15/tm). New Gold layer. -- fixed in 48ab75d65, 5059800bf, 4e65dc1ef, c40fef136, 587ce1843, f70fe8a11 and 874625f49, by Paolo's numbering of 2026-10-04: HeartGold's TM01 to TM92 and HM01 to HM08 as retail, every giver unchanged; then the seventh generation's TMs HeartGold lacks, Wild Charge, Snarl, Nature Power, Dazzling Gleam and Confide on their own numbers and the other 22 in Gen 7's order, Work Up, Psyshock and Venoshock filling TM94, TM97 and TM98; then TM120 to TM148, the 29 later TM moves in Scarlet and Violet's order; no TRs, no Tera Blast, Fly, Surf and Waterfall HMs only. One table answers every machine question (48ab75d65); the other 184 machine items are items with no use; who learns what keeps today's rule; the TM case has 156 places, a fifth save layout whose conversion makes an older save's machines New Gold's by move or drops them, 108 of the 240 mapping (4e65dc1ef); TM93 to TM148 describe their own moves on their type's disc (c40fef136), an item ball's are found as machines (587ce1843), and savedit reads and writes them (f70fe8a11, 874625f49). tm_save_conversion.json loads an older save's hg-engine machines as New Gold's and fails with the conversion reverted, and machine_past_100_from_the_bag.json teaches TM148 Upper Hand from the bag (4d36732aa)
- **low** *the item prices were Scarlet and Violet's, a TM's by its number* -- the item import (63fbeab4b) brought hg-engine's prices for every item both trees share: a Potion 200, an Ultra Ball 800, the Amulet Coin 30000, and at Celadon TM15 Hyper Beam 1600 and TM78 Captivate 32000. hg-engine's, not konefr's. Decision. Found by the TM proposal. New Gold layer. -- fixed in 71e467d2f, by Paolo's decision of 2026-10-04: the 514 items HeartGold has cost what they cost in HeartGold, 269 records and all 92 TMs among them, the items it lacks keeping theirs, and the importer keeps them; 727dfbd79 prices TM93 to TM148 at their shop step's price
- **low** *nothing sold or gave TM93 to TM148* -- Decision. Found by the TM proposal. New Gold layer. -- fixed in 1f5b24ccb, by Paolo's decision of 2026-10-04 on the shop proposal (.rounds/round15/tm/PROPOSTA-NEGOZIO-MT.md): Goldenrod's 5F TM clerk sells HeartGold's twelve and seven more every two badges, Johto's and Kanto's counted, eight steps at 1500, 2000, 3000, 4000, 5000, 6000, 8000 and 10000, so 12, 19, 26 and 68 TMs at 0, 2, 4 and 16 badges, no script or save byte changed; tm_shop_goldenrod.json, from the chain's Goldenrod save with two badges, buys TM094 Work Up on page 3 for 1500 and finds the next page's second place empty, which with four badges holds TM095 and fails it

New Gold layer. -- fixed in cf6737e50, by Paolo's decision of 2026-10-04, as from the fifth generation: a TM skips the quantity screen, and one in the bag gets HeartGold's own "You already have this!" (msg_0435 row 26, the Pokeathlon card shop's); tm_shop_goldenrod.json refuses TM70, held, and TM094 once bought

Found by the background chain, and in Paolo's list of 2026-10-05. -- fixed in b683d0eb7 (04j's Mareep a leg of its own, 04jb, a hunt), be0ecdf66 and 4bab0aa33: the whole chain on 72316be8f, 39 legs from a new game to Whitney's badge, passed with leg 08c's 5c0179b0f (.rounds/chain/72316be8f.log and its two parts)
- **low** *the rival's Azalea teams: a Larvitar at Lv 10 among Lv 22s, and the Bayleef team without Teddiursa* -- TRAINER_RIVAL_SILVER, _7 and _10 (#1, #266, #269): Misdreavus, Zubat and the starter at 22 and a Larvitar at 10 in each, a Teddiursa at 22 only beside Quilava and Croconaw. konefr's (e0bf78b7a), who is still finishing his trainers; KONEFR-NOTES, Allenatori 9. - **low** *added fronts the reference misplaces float or sink* -- konefr's call note "trubbish offset sprite (forse tutti nuovi?)", Paolo's point 1 of 2026-10-04. Trubbish was right, three rows over the ground line like retail's Pikachu, Poliwag and Metapod with the same shadow, and as in konefr's game; it sank only in the port before 6c562886d. Working every added front's place out from the data, and matching it against the 1039 frames of round 11's battle walk, found 158 records of the reference's own misplaced: 120 species and forms drawn with its placeholder, Bulbasaur's front, floating 21 or 22 rows up (Naclstack, Bramblin, the Iron Paradoxes, the Pikachu caps, the Gigantamax forms, the new megas), ten grounded species in the air (Tirtouga 18 rows, Iron Treads 14, Steenee and Revavroom 11, Clawitzer 9, Orthworm 8, Terapagos Terastal 8, Arctovish 6, Eiscue 5, Therian Enamorus 4), and forms not standing as their bases (Combat Breed Tauros, Dawn Wings Necrozma, Hisuian Braviary, Castform's forms); hg-engine's data/SpriteOffsets.c. Found by r15-sprites. -- fixed in 3b50ddc9e, in the importer: a picture another species drew first stands as that species, the ten take the median of retail's grounded fronts with their shadow, a form stands as its base; test_picture_tables fails on the old archive and with Trubbish at its old -20, and added_fronts_stand.json reads a wild Steenee, Naclstack and Trubbish standing 0, -1 and 3 rows over the line by scene.py's front1.lift (55c538108), 11 and 22 on the old archive

Found by r15-sprites. -- open
- **low** *Imposter acts when gained in battle or when Neutralizing Gas leaves* -- TryAbilityOnEntry's case 18 asks only imposterFlag, which a switch-in clears, and Imposter has no ABILITY_FLAG_FAILS_SWAP: a Pokemon given it by Skill Swap transforms at the next entry check, and an Imposter held back by the gas acts once the gas leaves; Showdown's imposter "does not activate when Skill Swapped or when Neutralizing Gas leaves the field", and Sosia has it fail when the opposite place is empty, where the port waits. Spending the flag at the first entry pass would do, once simultaneous replacements are checked. Found by r15-abilities. -- open
- **low** *Wind Power charges as it comes in under a Tailwind already blowing* -- case 21, from the reference (SwitchInAbilityCheck.c:738); Showdown's windpower has no onStart, and Energia Eolica names only the Tailwind's start. Since 92827ccda a Pokemon that copies Wind Power under its side's Tailwind charges by the same rule. To confirm. Found by r15-abilities. -- open

From reading the code. Found by r15-items. -- open
- **low** *Recycle and Harvest go by the battler's place, not the Pokemon* -- recycleItem[battlerId] is not cleared on a switch: a Pokemon sent into a place can Recycle what another used up there, and one back in the other place cannot; Riciclo from the fifth generation belongs to the Pokemon and cannot bring back an ally's item. Retail and the reference (recycle_item[CLIENT_MAX]) alike; a recycled item also carries the hand's tag, not its owner's. Harvest (Raccolta) to check. Found by r15-items. -- open

- **info** *chain.sh records the commit as HEAD and '+changes'* -- it cannot tell which of a dirty tree's edits the ROM held. Devkit. Found by r15-lab. -- closed with a reason: a landing runs it in a clean tree at the pushed commit, as the devkit README says
- **info** *the parallel legs start from the last good run's saves* -- a leg changed in a round reaches the next leg only in the background chain. Devkit. Found by r15-lab. -- closed with a reason: Paolo's decision 8 of 2026-10-04, the legs in parallel during a round (NEWGOLD_CHAIN_FROM, e41d06cd2) and the whole chain from a new game once after each landing (tools/newgold/chain.sh)

Devkit. Found by the EV/IV trainer, checked reading chain.sh. -- fixed in 637656a84: chain.sh builds both HeartGold ROMs, plays the legs in the order their from links draw (72316be8f's run stopped at a renamed leg its older list named) and resumes at a leg (chain.sh DIR LEG), as .rounds/chain/run_chain.sh did; 12b's --train is the edit left

was fixed on r16-review-fix. "To confirm" waits on Pokemon Central or a
second look; "decision" on Paolo. Engine layer unless a row says otherwise.

## Differences from konefr's reference

### abilities

- **high** *Comatose / Purifying Salt / Flower Veil / Pastel Veil / Water Bubble / Thermal...* -- Six abilities the test lists as IMPLEMENTED have no status-immunity half here. A Comatose Komala can be burned, poisoned, paralysed, frozen or put to sleep, and Hex does not double against it. Purifying Salt and grass-type Flower Veil... -- fixed in ec42c85dc, 89048363c, 0249a9c45 and b4da34137
- **high** *Prankster Dark-type immunity* -- A Prankster-boosted status move aimed at an opposing Dark type lands here. In the reference it "doesn't affect" the target. Only the priority half of Prankster is ported. -- fixed in 93831a0b8 (the priority half) and 550b1de93 (the Dark-type half, which 93831a0b8 had not done)
- **medium** *Queenly Majesty / Dazzling / Armor Tail: which priority they block* -- Here only a move-table priority above 0 is blocked. The reference blocks whatever adjusted priority the turn order used, which includes Prankster +1, Gale Wings +1, Triage +3 and Grassy Glide in grass. Because the reference stores it as a... -- fixed in e96373647: a priority above 0 after the turn order's adjustments is refused, canonically; the reference also refuses negative priority because it reads the value unsigned, a defect not copied (Paolo, 2026-09-23: reference defects in ability code are fixed)
- **medium** *Tinted Lens multiplier* -- In konefr's game Tinted Lens multiplies resisted damage by 1.25. Here it doubles it. -- fixed in 8a5135105 and, in the damage chain, da49bad71: the canonical x2 (Paolo, 2026-09-23)
- **medium** *Neutralizing Gas, Quick Draw, Supersweet Syrup: behaviour the reference doesn't...* -- In konefr's game these three abilities do nothing (the only reference reads of Neutralizing Gas are in Skill Swap/Role Play blocklists). Here Neutralizing Gas suppresses every other ability on the field, Quick Draw goes first 30% of the... -- fixed in 5cc4657ac, 9065e8fd1, 69e9b0307, 7d65064ea (the games' list and the Ability Shield) and 32f889c10 (Cud Chew)
- **medium** *Ice Scales stacking* -- konefr's game quarters special damage against Ice Scales in a single battle (two iterations) and cuts it to 1/16 in a double. The port halves it once. That breaks the port's own rule, which keeps the reference's Tablets/Vessel of Ruin and... -- fixed in 96aecbd40, with the Ruin and Fairy Aura copies in 2ea835b90 and 6e32d1091
- **medium** *Sand Rush / Sand Force sandstorm immunity* -- Sand Rush and Sand Force holders take sandstorm chip damage here. In the reference they are immune. Both are listed as IMPLEMENTED. -- fixed in a39e0941c
- **medium** *Infiltrator: Safeguard and Mist* -- Here Infiltrator gets past substitutes and screens, but Safeguard still blocks its status moves and Mist still blocks its stat drops. In the reference it gets past all four. -- fixed in 91661b739

### battle-mechanics

- **high** *AI move scoring* -- The trainer AI is still assembly. It reads a move's power and effect at ctx+0x3E1 / ctx+0x3DE + move*16, i.e. trainerAIData.moveData[], which is sized NUM_MOVES+1 = 468 (MOVE_SHADOW_FORCE). For every added move (ids 468..923) the AI reads... -- fixed in cd953fc55, cd60f9ab5, 7a7d4d35b, f083fc484 (matching) and 71d621a8d
- **high** *priority* -- The importer parses priority with `\d+` and drops the sign. Circle Throw and Dragon Tail have priority +6 here against the reference's -6, and Beak Blast and Shell Trap +3 against -3. So these phazing and charge moves act first instead of... -- fixed in 93831a0b8

### level-cap-exp

- **high** *experience formula (scaled, EXPERIENCE_FORMULA_GEN = GEN_LATEST = 9)* -- The reference truncates sqrt(2L+10) and sqrt(L+Lp+10) to whole numbers; ours computes them almost exactly. Almost every experience award therefore differs from the reference's. -- fixed in 7446d9129

- **medium** *Mark's and Nelson's defeat lines* -- a double battle prints the trainer's defeat line from TRMSG_DBL_LOSE_1, and both have only TRMSG_LOSE, so "I was wrong." and "Ooh, your Pokémon have potential." are never shown. -- fixed in 247823e32 (KONEFR-NOTES.md, Allenatori 7): the line retyped, hg-engine's rule for a no-partner double
- **medium** *a double battle without a partner, seen by sight* -- the port's sight check asked TrainerNumIsDouble, so Mark #395 on Route 36 went looking for a partner too; once it asked for a partner (0b3eaef4c) a player with one usable Pokemon was engaged and the double battle's setup asserted. The reference engages that player by sight and by talking and starts the double with one Pokemon. -- fixed in 18dab19df and 8d0f059b4 (matching), 0b3eaef4c and 16430cd42 (KONEFR-NOTES.md, Allenatori 7): a partner walks up only for DOUBLE_BATTLE, and a trainer who fights a double does not see a player who cannot fight it, retail's rule for every double; a departure from the reference, since this port's battle cannot run that double. With 757cb7ff3 underneath, Mark and Nelson played to the end in both fainting orders with no assert; mark_double_to_the_end.json (2a4adf251)
- **low** *Galarian names in capitals* -- 77469fbd4 names Galarian Slowbro, Farfetch'd and Slowking "SLOWBRO", "FARFETCHD" and "SLOWKING", their bases' SPECIES_ identifiers, so Nelson sends out "SLOWBRO". -- fixed in 147c9e1b8 and 590be859f (KONEFR-NOTES.md, Testi 1): a form so named takes its base's name, up a chain of forms
- **low** *Samantha's lines name a Meowth she no longer has* -- eb4e20f17 turns Beauty Samantha #70's Meowth into a Persian with its moves and leaves her lines saying MEOWTH. -- fixed in 5a6655bbc (KONEFR-NOTES.md, Allenatori 13): MEOWTH -> PERSIAN in both lines
- **low** *leg 12b raises the bot's team to 36 before Whitney* -- the playthrough reaches Whitney's badge only with savedit --train 36: the bot's team, at 23 to 30, lost to her ten times (five at the cap), her Farigiraf's Hyper Voice ending it. Paolo (2026-10-06): a balance question, for the trainers' and the AI's work after the port. Found landing the sixteenth round. -- open, after the port
